【三星電子拿下博通2000億美元大單】三星電子獲得一份價值逾2000億美元的合同，將向博通供應晶片，兩家公司正努力在人工智慧(AI)基礎設施市場爭取更大份額。三星電子週六在一份宣告中稱，這項為期五年的協議將持續至2030年，重點圍繞為博通產品提供三星2奈米及以下先進製程工藝。協議將深化兩公司在儲存晶片和晶圓代工技術領域的戰略合作。三星表示，在儲存晶片領域，將支援博通的下一代AI加速器落地。雙方的合作預計將擴充套件至基於三星2奈米工藝的先進封裝技術，包括2.3D和2.5D整合技術，以打造更高效能、更節能的AI和網路晶片。

### 2026-07-25 17:56:55  #其他

【水利部針對7省區啟動洪水防禦Ⅳ級應急響應】據預報，受今年第12號颱風“紅霞”帶來的強降雨影響，7月25日至7月30日，珠江流域北江、東江、韓江、粵東沿海榕江，長江流域中游支流漢江、洞庭湖水系湘江資水沅江澧水、鄱陽湖水系贛江，淮河流域上中游幹流及支流沙潁河、洪汝河等主要河流將出現明顯漲水過程，其中珠江流域東江可能發生編號洪水；暴雨區內部分中小河流可能發生超警以上洪水，山丘區山洪災害風險較高。  依據《水利部水旱災害防禦應急響應工作規程》，水利部於7月25日16時針對福建、江西、河南、湖北、湖南、廣東、廣西等省（自治區）啟動洪水防禦Ⅳ級應急響應，要求地方水利部門和相關流域管理機構密切監視天氣變化，加密雨水情監測預報，及時釋出預警資訊，強化會商研判和值班值守，科學精細排程防洪工程，突出抓好水庫安全度汛、中小河流洪水和山洪災害防禦等工作，及時提請地方政府提前轉移受威脅群眾和危險區人員，確保人民群眾生命財產安全。目前，5個水利部工作組、專家組正在廣東、福建等地臺風暴雨洪水防禦一線協助指導。

### 2026-07-25 17:50:49  #國際

【烏克蘭尋求與加拿大簽署為期30年的能源協議】烏克蘭第一副總理兼能源部長傑尼斯·什梅加爾表示，烏克蘭正尋求與加拿大簽署一項為期30年的戰略能源協議，並提議建立以加拿大液化天然氣供應為重點的“能源安全橋樑”。 什梅加爾表示，他與加拿大能源和自然資源部長蒂姆·霍奇森討論了天然氣和核能領域的合作。 作為會談的一部分，雙方討論了加拿大向烏克蘭提供6.5億加元（約合4.61億美元）直接贈款的可能性，以幫助烏克蘭購買冬季天然氣，這是加拿大對烏克蘭更廣泛援助計劃的一部分。什梅加爾表示，烏克蘭還邀請加拿大供應商在其地下設施儲存天然氣，並尋求加拿大製造商提供能源裝置。雙方還討論了小型模組化反應堆（SMR）領域的合作，基輔希望將這一領域納入擬議的長期協議，因為烏克蘭希望借鑑加拿大在該技術方面的經驗。

【大連首推二套房公積金貸款貼息 年內近90城發“房補”】近日，大連市推出階段性公積金貸款貼息新政，對2026年7月24日至2027年12月31日之間審批透過的二套房公積金貸款給予最長3年、實際利息15%的補貼，定向降低改善型置業群體還貸壓力。具體來算，對於新購置二套房的人來說，按照年利率3.075%、等額本息還款方式計算，一筆100萬元、30年期公積金二套房貸款，前3年可獲得利息補貼大約是13400元。對比來看，存量二套商業貸款辦理商轉公的群體獲益更為突出，借款人既可以享受前3年貼息，還能把較高利率的商業貸款置換為低利率公積金貸款，完整還款週期累計節省利息超3.29萬元，政策惠民效應更為明顯。放眼全國，年內已有近90座城市陸續出臺各類購房補貼、貸款減負舉措，地方穩樓市政策持續豐富工具箱，但針對二套房公積金貸款提供財政貼息的做法，大連屬於近期較為少見的探索。（中國之聲）

### 2026-07-25 13:50:35  #公司 #國際

【馬斯克點贊黃仁勳首條X推文：全力支援黃仁勳】近日，全球市值最高上市公司英偉達掌門人黃仁勳入駐社交媒體平臺X（原推特），截至北京時間25日11時30分，其粉絲數量已達到62.7萬。 黃仁勳的首條推文是一封英偉達參與聯署的信件，強調了開源人工智慧模型的重要性。該信由英偉達、微軟、Meta、IBM、戴爾科技、Linux基金會、a16z、Y Combinator、Mistral AI等數十家機構聯名簽署。 信中指出，美國發展AI經濟不能只依賴少數幾家封閉前沿模型公司，應支援開放權重模型發展，透過擴大AI可獲得性、促進競爭和增強安全性，讓AI技術擴散到整個經濟體系。 所謂“美國AI領導力”的衡量標準，不應只是擁有某一個最先進AI模型，而應看美國是否建立了一個強大、開放，並能夠擴散到各行業的生態系統。 開源模型能夠強化競爭，而競爭能夠確保AI帶來的收益被廣泛共享，而不是集中在少數企業手中。 公開信同時承認開放權重存在風險，例如模型釋出後可能脫離開發者控制，被修改或濫用，但解決方案不是限制開放模型，而是增強安全研究和防禦能力。 對此，特斯拉、SpaceX、Neuralink等多家前沿科技公司創始人馬斯克轉文稱，“老黃是對的，我全力支援”。

【棗莊財金“一把手”接掌*ST雅博】7月24日晚間，*ST雅博（002323.SZ）釋出公告稱，公司董事長張宗輝因到達法定退休年齡，近日已提交書面辭職報告，辭去第七屆董事會董事長及董事職務，其原定任期至第七屆董事會屆滿。公開資料顯示，張宗輝生於1966年，曾任棗莊市市中區區委常委、政法委書記；2019年5月至今任山東泉興能源集團有限公司黨委書記、董事長；2022年3月至今擔任山東雅博科技股份有限公司董事長。同一天，*ST雅博召開第七屆董事會第六次會議，審議透過了《關於選舉董事長的議案》，選舉楊建東為公司新任董事長，任期自董事會審議透過之日起至公司第七屆董事會任期屆滿之日止。簡歷顯示，楊建東生於1968年，歷任棗莊市財金控股集團有限公司總經理、黨委副書記；2021年3月至今兼任山東泉興科技有限公司董事兼總經理；2024年5月至今任棗莊市財金控股集團有限公司董事長、黨委書記；2026年5月至今兼任山東精工電子科技股份有限公司董事；2022年3月至2026年7月兼任山東雅博科技股份有限公司副董事長。7月22日，介面新聞發表《*ST雅博“保殼”倒計時：三億營收紅線前夜，誰精準送來兩筆大單？》，*ST雅博於7月16日完成控股股東變更，棗莊財金集團以17.1145%的持股比例取代原控股股東山東泉興科技有限公司成為新任控股股東，公司實際控制人由棗莊市國資委變更為棗莊市財政局。此番易主並非外部資本的“抄底”，而是棗莊國資體系內部的一次深度調整。*ST雅博證券部相關負責人對介面新聞表示，新舊控股股東隸屬體系不同，泉興科技本身是做實體的，而棗莊財金集團是財政局旗下的投資平臺，在資金方面的支援力度相對會更大、更靈活一些。值得一提的是，在國資宣佈“接力”後短短兩個月時間內，站在退市邊緣的*ST雅博接連中標“救命”訂單，兩筆中標專案總金額約2.94億元，剛好可以幫助公司跨過3億元營收的“保殼紅線”。

### 2026-07-25 11:04:17  #公司 #國際

【華潤集團原黨委委員、副總經理韓嵩嚴重違紀違法被“雙開”】經中共中央批准，中央紀委國家監委對華潤（集團）有限公司原黨委委員、副總經理韓嵩嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。

經查，韓嵩喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，搞迷信活動；違反中央八項規定精神，多次接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和旅遊安排，接受管理和服務物件提供的出行服務；在職務晉升、職工錄用工作中為他人謀取利益；廉潔底線失守，違規收受禮品、消費卡，縱容、默許親屬違規取酬；違規干預和插手市場經濟活動；毫無紀法底線，將權力異化為謀取個人私利的工具，大搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在專案承攬、職務晉升等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。

韓嵩嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、工作紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經中央紀委常委會會議研究並報中共中央批准，決定給予韓嵩開除黨籍處分；由國家監委給予其開除公職處分；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。（央視新聞）

經查，肖傑喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，違規收受禮品，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請；在幹部選拔任用工作中為他人謀取利益；利用職權為親屬謀取利益；對所在單位超標準發放農機補貼問題負有重要領導責任；目無法紀，利用擔任“一把手”的職權大肆插手工程專案，搞權錢交易，利用職務便利為他人在補貼申領、專案承攬等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。

肖傑嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和工作紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經中央紀委常委會會議研究並報中共中央批准，決定給予肖傑開除黨籍處分；按規定取消其享受的待遇；終止其海南省第八次黨代會代表資格；收繳其違紀違法所得；將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。（央視新聞）

【人民日報：查處攜程壟斷案釋放反壟斷反內卷強烈訊號】《人民日報》刊文指出，7月25日，市場監管總局依法對攜程集團有限公司（以下簡稱攜程）濫用市場支配地位實施壟斷行為作出行政處罰，罰沒款合計51.79億元。

這規範警示所有平臺企業，無論是簡單直接的“二選一”，還是隱蔽複雜的“技術+生態+行為”複合型壟斷，都逃不過反壟斷利劍，監管部門絕不允許以任何形式濫用市場支配地位實施排除、限制競爭行為。

攜程壟斷案的查處是平臺經濟反壟斷常態化監管的又一重大標誌性案件，是新型壟斷行為首案，也是深入整治“內卷式”競爭，推動平臺企業和平臺內經營者、勞動者共贏發展的典型案例，對推動我國平臺經濟創新和健康發展，以及經濟社會高質量發展都具有重要意義。

此次發射於美東時間24日17時51分進行。火箭第一級“超級重型”助推器在墨西哥灣附近海域濺落，但因重新啟動的發動機數量少於預期，導致該級火箭以高於預期的速度濺落海面。

第二級“星艦”飛船飛行期間，6臺發動機執行正常，並部署20顆新一代“星鏈”衛星。飛船還在太空中短暫重啟一臺發動機，為未來登月任務所需的在軌點火能力進行驗證。在發射約1小時5分鐘後，飛船於澳大利亞西北方向的印度洋濺落。（央視新聞）

今日11大白酒單品六漲四跌一平，贏家佔據優勢。上漲方面，國窖1573的漲幅居前，上漲7元/瓶，連續兩日自近一月最低位反彈；五糧液普五八代上漲6元/瓶，終結此前三連陰，自“酒價內參”去年11月有統計以來的最低位處再度啟動修復；古井貢古20上漲5元/瓶，自昨日創下的7月份以來最低位反彈；青花汾20上漲3元/瓶，自近半個月最低位回升，重新站上370元關口；洋河夢之藍M6+與習酒君品均小漲1元/瓶，其中前者連續三日走升，進一步逼近600元關口，後者連續兩日回升，平近半個月最高價。下跌方面，精品茅臺與青花郎跌幅居前，均下跌4元/瓶，其中前者連續兩日回落，再度跌破2400元關口；後者結束此前兩連漲，但仍站穩700元大關。飛天茅臺與五糧液1618均下跌2元/瓶，其中前者的全國零售均價連續四日自提價後的階段高位小幅回撥，後者連續兩日回落，再度失守820元一線。水晶劍南春價格環比昨日保持不變。

【紅霞加強為颱風級】據中國氣象局中央氣象臺：25日7時，颱風“紅霞”（颱風級）中心位置為北緯20.5度、東經118.4度。參考位置為距離廣東省汕尾市東南方向約405公里。 中央氣象臺7月25日06時繼續釋出颱風橙色預警：今年第12號颱風“紅霞”已於今天（25日）早晨由強熱帶風暴級加強為颱風級，早晨5點鐘其中心位於廣東汕尾東南方向約450公里的南海東北部海面上，就是北緯20.3度、東經118.8度，中心附近最大風力有12級（33米/秒），中心最低氣壓為975百帕，七級風圈半徑150-280公里，十級風圈半徑50公里。 預計，“紅霞”將以每小時20公里左右的速度向西偏北方向移動，強度逐漸加強，並向粵東沿海靠近，今天夜間至26日早晨將在香港到廣東惠來一帶沿海登陸（35-42米/秒，12-14級，颱風級或強颱風級），登陸後轉向偏北方向移動，強度逐漸減弱。

【浙江台州排查非標電動車產銷鏈條：涉事單位停業整改】最近，總檯《每週質量報告》欄目曝光了浙江台州電動腳踏車產業鏈變相架空國家安全標準，違規生產、銷售超標準電動腳踏車問題。節目播出後，台州市市場監督管理局、市公安局交管支隊迅速組織人員，對全市電動車銷售門店、生產企業展開全面排查。截至目前，台州市共出動相關執法人員超過2500人次，檢查電動腳踏車及其配套產品生產企業60家，檢查電動車銷售單位兩千餘家次，已立案42件。7月22日，記者在報道涉及的浙江鑽豹電動車股份有限公司看到，企業已處於停業整改狀態。監管部門成立專案組對企業生產銷售等情況展開徹查，發現該企業確實存在違規生產問題。（央視新聞）

1、美國太空探索技術公司重型運載火箭“星艦”於美國中部時間24日下午從得克薩斯州南部的發射基地升空，實施第13次試飛。

3、葉門胡塞武裝控制下的葉門荷臺達地區遭空襲。沙特主導的多國聯軍隨後宣佈，對荷臺達的胡塞武裝軍事據點實施打擊，但未將荷臺達港口列為攻擊目標，並強調當地所有港口目前均對航運開放。
4、國際市場服務機構克普勒24日在社交媒體釋出訊息說，中東兩大關鍵海上通道23日船舶通航情況出現分化，霍爾木茲海峽通航量明顯下降，而連線紅海和亞丁灣的曼德海峽通航量有所回升。
5、美國總統特朗普在白宮談及對伊朗戰爭的“退出戰略”時表示，美國有兩種選擇：一是繼續當前軍事行動，並可能加大打擊力度，逐步摧毀伊朗的軍事能力；二是透過談判達成協議。特朗普同時表示，美方目前仍在與伊朗進行談判。
6、美英兩國計劃下週在英國首都倫敦召開一次高階別會議，重點討論組建一個旨在“保護霍爾木茲海峽海上航運”的國際聯盟。
7、WTI原油期貨9月合約結算價收跌3.12%，WTI主連本週累漲8.27%；布倫特原油期貨9月合約結算價收跌3.88%，本週累漲9.85%。
8、高通公司告知客戶，由於成本上漲，將不得不將產品價格上調兩位數百分比；此次漲價將適用於9月1日之後出貨的產品。

【兩家企業起訴美國新關稅措施違法】美國從24日起以所謂“強迫勞動”為名對數十個國家和地區加徵10%至12.5%的關稅。美國兩家小企業當天向美國國際貿易法院提起訴訟，認為這項新的關稅措施超出了美國政府徵收關稅的法定許可權。這兩家小企業的訴狀稱，美國政府依據《1974年貿易法》第301條，以所謂“強迫勞動”為名對數十個國家和地區加徵10%至12.5%的關稅，但美國政府並未就各國所謂的“強迫勞動”問題作出更加具體的事實認定，不足以為大範圍徵收關稅提供法律依據。訴狀還稱，301條款過去通常用於針對特定國家或特定行業，而此次依據301條款對數十個國家和地區大面積加徵關稅，並沒有歷史先例。原告認為，美國政府此舉實際上是在重新實施此前已被美國最高法院裁定違法的大規模加徵關稅措施。（央視新聞）

### 2026-07-25 04:57:04  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Gains on Trade Advantage — The Mexican peso strengthened to around 17.48 per USD in late July from 17.52 earlier in the month as investors viewed Mexico's trade position with the US as relatively favorable. The US announced new tariffs ranging from 10% to 12.5% on imports from roughly 60 economies. For Mexico, imports that do not qualify for preferential treatment under the USMCA will face a 10% tariff, while goods that comply with the agreement's rules of origin will remain exempt, preserving the country's preferential access to the US market. Existing exemptions also remain in place for products already covered by sector-specific tariffs, including automobiles, steel, aluminum, and pharmaceuticals. Mexico was included among the countries subject to the minimum 10% tariff, while many economies without trade agreements will face 12.5% duties, preserving Mexico's competitive advantage over several export rivals. The peso also found support from a pullback in global energy prices.

Vale fell 0.6% but still posted a weekly gain of more than 3%, supported by stronger-than-expected production figures released earlier in the week. US Stocks Mixed Amid Middle East Tensions — US equity indices closed mixed on Friday as investors assessed corporate earnings and the outlook for interest rates. ### 2026-07-25 04:05:22  #國際

【霍爾木茲海峽通航量下降 曼德海峽回升】國際市場服務機構克普勒24日在社交媒體釋出訊息說，中東兩大關鍵海上通道23日船舶通航情況出現分化，霍爾木茲海峽通航量明顯下降，而連線紅海和亞丁灣的曼德海峽通航量有所回升。克普勒資料顯示，霍爾木茲海峽23日共有6艘船舶完成通行，較前一日下降60%。除一艘船舶外，其餘均經由伊朗劃設的航道通行。與此同時，曼德海峽當天共有49艘船舶完成過境，高於此前水平。其中包括4艘關閉船舶自動識別系統進行“暗航”的船舶。克普勒表示，此前在亞丁灣和紅海因安全風險掉頭返航的部分船舶已恢復航行並完成通行，但仍有部分船舶在掉頭後繼續停留觀望。目前國際航運企業仍在持續評估地區安全形勢，整體通航活動呈現“謹慎但選擇性恢復”的特點。（新華社）

Discovery (WBD.O) — the suspension may extend until no later than June next year — while courts consider lawsuits filed by multiple state attorneys general seeking to block the deal. In court filings the parties said the agreement freezes the transaction and extends a shorter pause previously ordered by a California federal judge. The merger would combine major studios, streaming services HBO Max and Paramount+, and networks including CBS and CNN. Paramount CEO David Ellison led the acquisition and prevailed over Netflix after multiple rounds of higher bids.

【對沖基金做空加元創2024年8月以來新高】受美國加徵關稅威脅及美加貿易緊張局勢持續升級影響，對沖基金對加元的看跌押注達到兩年來最高水平。美國商品期貨交易委員會（CFTC）最新資料顯示，截至7月21日當週，槓桿資金管理人持有的加元淨空頭頭寸增至99,823份合約，創下自2024年8月以來的最高紀錄。在貿易摩擦加劇的背景下，加元正經歷五週以來最糟糕的單週表現。本週加元兌美元匯率累計下跌0.5%，市場悲觀情緒顯著升溫。本週早些時候，特朗普政府宣佈對部分加拿大商品加徵50%的關稅，以回應加拿大對美國酒類、汽車和乳製品採取的貿易限制措施。這一舉措進一步加劇了加元的貶值壓力。今年以來，受加拿大國內經濟疲軟以及美加兩國不利的利差影響，加元表現持續落後於大多數主要貨幣。隨著貿易不確定性上升，市場對加元的負面預期仍在發酵。

【半導體封測產能供不應求 預付款模式延伸至第三方廠商】近日，安靠科技與英偉達達成一項總價值15億美元的戰略合作，引發市場關注。根據協議，英偉達將向安靠科技提供一筆預付款，以支援其在美國的先進封裝產能擴建。對此，業內人士在接受記者採訪時表示：以往依託預付款鎖定長期產能的商業模式，大多出現在頭部科技企業與臺積電的合作框架之內；此次英偉達主動鎖定安靠科技的先進封測產能，直觀印證全球AI算力需求持續高景氣，臺積電先進封裝產能在中長期將持續處於供不應求的緊平衡狀態。業內人士還表示，伴隨國產AI晶片產業化落地提速的發展大勢，國內先進封測行業迎來技術迭代與規模升級的全新視窗期，建議具備前瞻佈局思維的封測廠商及封裝裝置、材料企業，應提前佈局資本投入與產線建設，精準承接AI產業爆發帶來的紅利。(上海證券報)

### 2026-07-25 03:18:09  #其他

【“四流”一致成硬門檻 銀行對大宗商品貿易融資稽核邏輯變了】企業進行虛假貿易、“空轉走單”等，合同流、資金流、發票流或許還能湊齊，但貨物流往往是缺失的。“四流”中缺了關鍵“一流”，稅收和信貸的合規性也經不起追問。因此，貨物流成了大宗商品信貸審批嚴查的重點。4月末，國家稅務總局釋出《納稅人合規開具發票正負面清單》（下稱“清單”），將開展融資性貿易、虛假貿易，“四流”（合同流、貨物流/服務流、資金流、發票流）不一致，無合理商業目的“對開、環開”發票，虛構交易環節“空轉走單”等28種情形列為違規開票負面清單。“清單”落地後，銀行業快速針對大宗商品貿易融資業務完善風控規則。長三角某城商行下發專項管控通知，對大宗商品質押和貿易融資全流程升級風控。“清單”落地至今已滿三個月，糾治效應沿著“貿易—發票—信貸”的鏈條逐級傳導，合規經營實體貿易企業獲得了更多金融活水。(上海證券報)

### 2026-07-25 03:16:10  #觀點

【利用AI證券詐騙引關注 券商密集釋出風險提示】當前，利用AI的非法證券活動引發關注。據記者不完全統計，7月以來，已有銀河證券、國盛證券、中泰證券、華福證券、華鑫證券等近10家券商釋出相關風險提示，涉及仿冒App、虛假量化交易、AI薦股騙局等多類詐騙手段。梳理近期出現的案例不難發現，證券詐騙行為已利用AI，主要表現為兩種形態：一種是將AI作為營銷噱頭，用“智慧量化”“機器人穩賺”等概念包裝騙局；另一種是將AI作為工具，用於批次偽造人像、研報、交易場景。二者共同指向一個現實：當不法分子完成技術升級，傳統的防騙體系正面臨新的挑戰。記者從業內瞭解到，在AI偽造薦股騙局中，直播間裡聲情並茂的“投資專家”，通常就是依靠這類低價程式合成。不法分子藉助AI數字人搭建虛假直播場景，AI換臉復刻知名分析師的面孔，語音克隆模仿從業者的聲線，生成式AI批次炮製帶有機構公章的虛假檔案、投資協議、盈利截圖，從而獲取投資者信任。(上海證券報)

【摩根士丹利：SpaceX股價若跌至100美元 將意味著AI業務估值歸零】摩根士丹利分析師指出，近期市場對SpaceX股票的拋售已將其股價推至接近人工智慧（AI）業務零估值的水平。該機構認為，當前市場悲觀情緒與公司基本面之間出現的顯著脫節，為投資者提供了具有吸引力的佈局視窗。自6月中旬完成創紀錄的860億美元首次公開募股（IPO）以來，SpaceX股價走勢持續承壓。該股在上市初期短暫衝高後一路下挫，本週早些時候一度觸及110.85美元的低位，較IPO發行價下跌18%。摩根士丹利分析師亞當·喬納斯（Adam Jonas）在7月25日釋出的最新研報中強調，許多投資者預計，隨著首批限售股於下月解禁，SpaceX股價可能進一步下探至每股100美元。他指出，若股價跌至該水平，意味著投資者不僅完全無視其AI業務的價值，甚至給出了負估值。喬納斯重申了對SpaceX股票的“增持”評級，並維持300美元的目標價。在採用分部估值法測算中，超過50%的估值來自公司的AI業務。他表示，多數投資者對SpaceX旗下Grok和Cursor等AI資產大幅折價，主要擔憂AI業務資本支出高企、經濟效益存在不確定性，且需要管理層投入大量精力。

【王毅會見伊朗外長阿拉格齊】中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅24日在吉爾吉斯斯坦出席上海合作組織外長會期間會見伊朗外長阿拉格齊。
王毅表示，近期中東海灣局勢再度緊張升級，中方對此深表關切。一個多月前簽署的諒解備忘錄是多方斡旋調停的寶貴成果，寄託著伊朗和中東人民止戰安邦的和平期許，不應前功盡棄。談判的大門既然開啟就不應關上，和平只要還有希望就不應放棄。當務之急是堅持談判化解爭端方向，迴歸落實備忘錄共識。作為全面戰略伙伴，中方堅定支援伊朗維護自身主權、安全和民族尊嚴，將秉持習近平主席提出的維護和促進中東和平穩定四點主張，繼續支援巴基斯坦等各方斡旋努力，儘早恢復當事方對話談判，妥處霍爾木茲海峽問題，推進鄰國間關係改善，共謀海灣國家集體安全，為早日恢復中東地區和平安寧作出貢獻。
阿拉格齊通報了中東海灣地區最新形勢，表示美方違反雙方達成的諒解備忘錄，導致局勢緊張升級。伊方談判意願從未改變，也不希望衝突持續下去，敦促美方信守承諾，保持克制，與伊方相向而行，尋找合理解決方案。在當前關鍵時刻，伊中作為全面戰略伙伴，加強溝通協調至關重要。感謝中方始終秉持客觀公正立場，一以貫之勸和止戰，期待中方繼續發揮重要作用，推動局勢走向緩和降溫。（新華社）

Meanwhile, President Donald Trump warned he was considering an unprecedented military response against Iran and vowed severe retaliation if Tehran backed further Houthi attacks on vessels in the Red Sea.

### 2026-07-24 23:21:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #Canada #Currency #Importance 1

Canadian Dollar Steady on Softer Inflation — The Canadian dollar was at 1.41 per USD, holding most of the pullback from the one-month low of 1.40 on July 17th after a soft PPI added to the disinflationary momentum in Canada. Canada's producer prices fell 1.4% month-over-month in June 2026, the sharpest decline since December 2023. Meanwhile, annual consumer inflation eased to 2.8% in June from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. The Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis are not spreading broadly through the economy. The softer inflation data reduced expectations of further Bank of Canada rate hikes this year, limiting the yield advantage that has supported the Canadian dollar.

### 2026-07-24 22:49:31  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Canadian Bond Yield Eases as Oil Rally Stalls — The yield on Canada's 10-year government bond fell to about 3.60% from an over one-month high of 3.66% on July 23rd as the oil rally paused, easing energy-driven inflation concerns. Meanwhile, Canada's annual inflation rate eased to 2.8% in June 2026 from 3.2% in May, slightly below forecasts of 2.9%. Gasoline prices increased at a slower pace, while the Bank of Canada's preferred core inflation measures fell to their lowest levels in more than five years, reinforcing the BoC's view that higher energy costs stemming from the Middle East oil supply crisis are not spreading broadly throughout the economy. The softer inflation data also reduced expectations of further Bank of Canada interest rate hikes this year.

### 2026-07-24 22:45:20  #國際

【APEC人工智慧高階別論壇關於促進亞太地區人工智慧發展的宣告】 2026年7月24日，中方在四川省成都市主辦APEC人工智慧高階別論壇，論壇主題為“促進人工智慧普惠包容發展”。APEC成員經濟體重申，我們致力於在人工智慧領域深化交流、凝聚共識、加強合作、促進創新，並致力於繼續實施《2040年APEC布特拉加亞願景》、《APEC網際網路和數字經濟路線圖》和《APEC人工智慧倡議（2026—2030）》。

考慮到APEC成員經濟體的多樣化需求，我們鼓勵各經濟體開展經驗分享，推動人工智慧在亞太地區負責任的發展和應用，並期待在以下人工智慧領域促進合作：

促進人工智慧安全發展和部署。我們鼓勵推動安全優先的人工智慧發展，促進旨在應對風險、造福人民的創新。鼓勵各成員經濟體就促進發展可靠、可及、可擴充套件、可信、安全且有韌性的人工智慧基礎設施，以及提升數字基礎設施可及性交流最佳實踐。我們將促進資料跨境流動，這對人工智慧發展至關重要，同時增強消費者和企業信任。

加快人工智慧的負責任普及應用。我們鼓勵以多種形式推進人工智慧賦能經濟發展，為人工智慧融入經濟社會各領域營造有利環境。我們推動面向社會各群體提供可及的教育和培訓資源，提高人工智慧素養和技能。我們認識到，人工智慧在促進貿易、推動海關程式現代化和提升企業競爭力方面的作用日益增強，並鼓勵進一步交流這些領域的最佳實踐，支援經濟增長。

營造有利於人工智慧創新的環境。我們讚賞採取以市場為導向、以創新為驅動的政策，促進人工智慧發展和廣泛應用。我們鼓勵成員經濟體考慮採取兼顧風險應對和負責任應用的人工智慧政策。在尊重安全、資料保護和智慧財產權的同時，我們鼓勵各成員經濟體支援在開發和部署過程中採用強大安全保障措施的開源模型和專案。

推動廣泛參與、普惠共享的APEC合作。我們歡迎加強亞太地區成員經濟體之間的能力建設和知識分享，並透過開放對話和協同行動，包括同公共部門和私營部門開展合作，促進創新和經濟增長。我們將繼續支援所有APEC成員經濟體參與人工智慧驅動型經濟，並使人工智慧最大限度惠及全體民眾。

【2026年亞太經合組織人工智慧高階別論壇在四川成都舉辦】作為2026年亞太經合組織（APEC）數字周重要活動之一，7月24日，2026年APEC人工智慧高階別論壇在四川省成都市舉辦。中國國家網際網路資訊辦公室主任莊榮文，四川省委書記、省人大常委會主任王曉暉，2026年APEC高官會主席陳旭，APEC秘書處執行主任佩德羅薩，四川省委常委、成都市委書記曹立軍出席論壇開幕式並致辭。中國國家網際網路資訊辦公室副主任王京濤主持開幕式。莊榮文表示，2025年11月，在韓國慶州舉辦的APEC會議上，習近平主席對中方以擔任2026年APEC東道主為契機，著力推進人工智慧等務實合作發表了重要意見。一週前，習近平主席出席2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式並發表主旨講話，為全球人工智慧發展和治理進一步指明瞭方向。中方舉辦本次論壇，旨在積極踐行習近平主席重要講話精神，深化交流對話，凝聚廣泛共識，促進人工智慧朝著向上向善、造福人類的方向發展。我們願與各方一道，堅持創新驅動，協同加強科技創新，鼓勵開展“人工智慧+”行動，推動人工智慧共同繁榮；堅持普惠共享，分享發展經驗，彌合數智鴻溝，釋放人工智慧發展紅利；堅持築牢底線，推動構建法律法規、技術監測、風險預警、應急響應體系，確保人工智慧安全可控；堅持文明互鑑，推動優秀文化資源數字化、智慧化傳播，彰顯人工智慧賦能作用；堅持共商共建，推動建設具有廣泛共識的人工智慧治理框架和標準規範，完善人工智慧全球治理。面對機遇挑戰，中國堅持統籌發展和安全，推動人工智慧向上向善、安全可控、造福人民，併為全球人工智慧發展和治理積極貢獻力量。面向未來，我們將繼續在人工智慧領域擔當大國責任、貢獻中國方案，做創新發展的推動者、安全可控的守護者、文明互鑑的促進者、全球治理的參與者，推動人工智慧實現更好發展。

### 2026-07-24 22:32:57  #其他

【浙江多家銀行密集下調存款利率 什麼訊號？】近日，個別地區城農商行密集調整存款利率引起市場關注。據記者瞭解，近期浙江當地多家銀行有對定期存款利率的下調動作，部分銀行調整了掛牌利率上浮後的執行利率，也有部分銀行更新了掛牌利率。 進入下半年，市場密切關注增量貨幣政策取向，上述資訊引發猜想。不過，受訪人士普遍認為，上述調整僅為區域性自律機制調整，不應理解為降息前的訊號。 進入下半年，市場密切關注增量貨幣政策取向。作為城農商行較為密集的省份，浙江地區銀行上述動作引發部分市場猜想，包括其他省份會否跟進、是否為全面降息前奏等。不過，多位受訪人士對記者表示，上述調整僅為正常的區域性自律機制調整，無需過度解讀。在利率市場化背景下，其他地區的利率自律機制也會有類似動態調整，只是未被集中關注。（第一財經）

TSX Nears Record High on Mining Gains — The S&P/TSX Composite Index rose nearly 0.5% to trade above 35,000 on Friday, nearing record highs as mining stocks advanced. Gold prices rose, lifting miners, with Agnico Eagle and Barrick gaining about 1% each, WPM rising more than 1.5%, and Teck Resources extending gains by nearly 2% after beating profit estimates. Meanwhile, the oil rally paused, easing energy-driven inflation concerns. Lower bond yields supported financials and the broader market, with BMO, Scotiabank, and Brookfield gaining more than 0.5%. In technology, Constellation Software rose 1.5% and Shopify added nearly 0.5%, while Celestica fell about 5.5%, tracking a selloff in US chipmakers. Energy stocks traded higher as oil prices remained elevated despite the recent pullback. Canadian Natural added 0.5%, while Cenovus edged up ahead of its earnings report. Canadian National Railway, down 0.8%, is also set to report results today.

US Services Activity Rises Most in 8 Months — The S&P Global US Services PMI rose to 53.6 in July of 2026 from 51.2 in the previous month, marking the fastest expansion in services sector activity so far this year, according to a flash estimate. Output was supported by a larger intake of new work for companies, with companies noting a pickup in activity due to FIFA World Cup spending and higher investments in sales, marketing, and product development. Still, the impact of the war in Iran, mainly though higher energy prices that lifted operating cars and squeezed consumers' purchasing power, continued to impact activity levels. Therefore, employment growth was only marginal. Input prices for the sector grew to a 14-month high, and output inflation surged to its highest in nearly four years. Despite inflationary risks, the sentiment gauge rose to a one-year high.

Treasury Yields Edge Down on Friday — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note edged lower to 4.67% on Friday, pausing after a four-session rally that had lifted the benchmark yield to its highest level since January 2025. Oil prices eased during the session, helping push yields slightly lower, although tensions in the Middle East remained elevated, with US President Trump saying he would soon decide whether to launch a "massive attack" on Iran. On the data front, flash S&P Global PMIs showed that US services sector activity strengthened in July, while manufacturing growth slowed and price pressures intensified. Investors are now turning their attention to next week's Federal Reserve policy decision. The central bank is widely expected to leave the federal funds rate unchanged, although markets are pricing in roughly a 35% probability of a rate hike. The probability of a September hike currently stands at nearly 80%.

【25家科技巨頭聯名簽署公開信，力挺開放權重AI模型】包括IBM、Meta Platforms、英偉達、微軟在內的25家美國科技公司聯名簽署公開信，呼籲開放權重AI模型。該公開信指出，開源權重模型對健康的AI生態系統至關重要。美國在人工智慧領域面臨開放權重的選擇。誠然，開放權重模型確實存在真實且獨特的風險。但事實上，開放可能是實現AI安全與保障的最重要路徑之一。僅僅依賴封閉模型本身並非安全：它們可能被攻破、被濫用，或以外部無法察覺的方式失效。開放權重模型則允許更廣泛的研究人員和開發者社群檢查其行為、識別漏洞、制定防護措施並隨時間推移不斷改進它們。

【25 U.S. tech firms urge open AI model weights】Twenty-five U.S. technology companies, including IBM, Meta Platforms, NVIDIA and Microsoft, signed a joint letter urging open model weights. The letter says open-weight models are critical to a healthy AI ecosystem and that the U.S. faces a choice on openness. It acknowledges real, unique risks from openness but argues that openness may be essential to AI safety, since closed models can be hacked, misused or fail in ways undetectable externally. Open weights, the letter says, allow broader researcher and developer scrutiny to identify vulnerabilities, build defenses and iteratively improve models.

Chile Producer Price Growth Eases in June — Chile's producer prices rose 19.7% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 20.5% increase in May. Mining prices recorded the strongest gain, rising 32.5% after a 35% increase in the previous month, driven by a 32.9% jump in copper prices. Manufactured goods prices increased 5.5%, accelerating from 4.8% in May, while utility prices rose 5.2% after a 5% gain. On a monthly basis, producer prices advanced 0.6%, slowing from a 2.8% increase in May, bringing the year-to-date rise to 8.8%.

### 2026-07-24 21:48:56  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Composite PMI #Importance 2

US Business Activity Growth Accelerates to 8-Month High — The S&P Global US Composite PMI rose to 53.6 in July 2026 from 51.9 in June, marking its highest level since November and signaling a stronger expansion in private sector activity after near-stagnation in March. Growth was driven by the services sector, where business activity accelerated to an 8-month high on stronger inflows of new orders. Manufacturing output continued to expand but at a much slower pace, with factories reporting the weakest increases in production and new orders since March. The stronger overall performance encouraged firms to increase hiring for the first time in 3 months, while business confidence climbed to its highest level in 8 months. However, supply chain pressures intensified, with supplier delivery times deteriorating to the greatest extent in nearly four years amid ongoing conflict in the Middle East. Inflationary pressures also strengthened, as input cost inflation accelerated to a 14-month high and selling price inflation approached a 4-year peak.

### 2026-07-24 21:48:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

US Manufacturing Growth Eases Slightly in July — The S&P Global US Manufacturing PMI edged down to 53.8 in July 2026 from 53.9 in June, falling short of market expectations of 54.3, according to the preliminary estimate. Despite the slight decline, the index remained close to its highest levels in more than four years. The modest slowdown reflected a sharp easing in production growth, which weakened to its slowest pace since March, while new orders expanded at the weakest rate in four months. Slower inventory accumulation, following exceptionally strong stockbuilding in May and June, also weighed on the headline PMI. These headwinds were partly offset by a renewed increase in factory employment and longer supplier delivery times. Unlike the delays typically associated with strong demand, the latest deterioration in supplier performance was primarily linked to supply disruptions stemming from the Middle East.

US Equities Muted on Friday — US equity indices were muted on Friday, holding losses from last session as markets assessed earnings and risks of higher interest rates. The S&P 500 was flat, while the Nasdaq 100 inched down and the Dow inched up. Oil and product prices eased but remained sharply higher on the week as escalating strikes between Iran and the US halted tanker flows through the Persian Gulf and Red Sea. Treasury yields tracked the moves in energy prices as a portion of the market positioned for a Fed hike next week. Intel fell nearly 1% despite posting its highest revenue growth in 17 years, tracking volatility in the chip sector following manufacturers' soaring valuations since the second quarter. Also, Amex sank 5% after posting their results. On the other hand, Verizon gained around 2% each on a subscriber growth beat. The Nasdaq 100 was due to close the week 0.5% lower amid concerns of AI overspending for hyperscalers. Tesla dropped 16%, Alphabet lost 9%, and Meta slipped 7% since last Friday.

【港交所上市新規今起生效：門檻降低、WVR放寬、保密遞表擴圍】7月24日，港交所正式釋出有關提升香港上市機制競爭力的建議諮詢總結，修訂後的《上市規則》即日生效。不同投票權（WVR）架構及第二上市申請人的市值門檻由100億港元降至60億港元，WVR架構的持股佔比要求同步放寬，保密遞表資格亦從特定型別企業擴充套件至所有新申請人。 此次門檻調整同步覆蓋WVR架構公司：若上市時市值達400億港元，不同投票權比率上限可進一步提高至20比1；對於市值在200億港元以上，或市值60億港元且最近一個會計年度收入達6億港元的WVR申請人，同樣適用新的財務資格標準。在WVR架構的適用範圍上，新規明確將業務模式創新的發行人納入WVR上市途徑，不再侷限於科技創新企業。合資格的生物科技和特專科技申請人將被自動視為“創新產業”公司。上市時不同投票權持股佔比要求亦有所放寬，若持股金額達40億港元，佔比要求可由10%降至5%。

### 2026-07-24 21:24:11  #國際

【中國駐泰國使館發言人就中泰聯合宣告涉臺內容答記者問】問：臺灣當局有關部門近日就《中華人民共和國政府和泰王國政府關於面向繁榮中泰命運共同體的聯合宣告》涉臺內容發表宣告，表示所謂“抗議”。請問中方對此有何評論？

答：臺灣是中國領土不可分割的一部分。中方堅決反對臺灣當局歪曲事實、混淆視聽、進行政治操弄。任何干擾中泰友好關係的圖謀，都不會得逞。

一箇中國原則是公認的國際關係基本準則，是國際社會普遍共識，也是中華人民共和國同建交國建立和發展外交關係的政治基礎和根本前提。中泰兩國1975年簽署《中華人民共和國和泰王國關於建立外交關係的聯合公報》，泰王國政府承認中華人民共和國政府是中國的唯一合法政府，承認中國政府關於只有一箇中國、臺灣是中國領土不可分割的一部分的立場。

此次中泰聯合宣告關於臺灣問題的表述，體現了泰國政府長期堅持一箇中國政策的一貫立場，反映了雙方共同維護中泰關係政治基礎的堅定意願。

中方相信泰國政府將繼續堅定奉行一箇中國政策，在涉及中國核心利益和重大關切問題上給予中方堅定支援。中方願同泰方繼續推動構建中泰命運共同體，更好造福兩國人民。（環球網）

### 2026-07-24 21:15:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #Belgium #Business Confidence #Importance 1

Belgium Business Pessimism Improves for Third Month — Belgium's business confidence indicator rose by 0.5 points to -11.9 in July 2026, up from -12.4 in the prior month. This marked the highest reading since the start of the year and the third consecutive monthly improvement, although below market expectations of -10.2. Sentiment strenghened most in the construction industry, where confidence rebounded to -3.0 from -12.0. Sentiment also edged higher in manufacturing (-15.5 vs. -15.7) and strengthened notably in business-related services (-3.8 vs. -7.4). In contrast, confidence in the trade sector deteriorated further to -10.8 from -9.3, reflecting weaker activity in textiles and electrical and electronic appliances. Meanwhile, confidence remained weak in building completion and finishing (-13.4 vs. -4.3) and structural building work (-5.0 vs. -4.2).

Canada New Housing Price Decline Slows — New housing prices in Canada fell 0.1% month-over-month in June 2026, following a 0.3% decline in May. The drop was smaller than the 0.2% decline expected by markets and marked the mildest monthly decrease since prices last increased in February. The decline was driven by a 0.3% fall in land prices, while house-only prices rose 0.1%. On an annual basis, new housing prices declined 2.3%, easing slightly from a 2.4% drop in May.

【國產工業小零件改寫全球產業鏈格局 專精特新小巨人企業實力出圈】今年上半年，規模以上專精特新“小巨人”工業企業增加值，同比增長10.4%，增長優勢突出。深耕細分領域的專精特新企業，是穩固產業鏈、推動製造業高質量發展的重要力量。 預埋螺套雖然只是單一金屬構件，單件重量就能達到十至二十多公斤，陸上、海上所有大功率風機的葉片錨固環節，都離不開它。某生產企業負責人表示：“大概15分鐘就能生產出一隻預埋螺套，一隻葉片大概用到300只到400只預埋螺套，能夠連線100米到200米左右長度的葉片。我們今年預計的訂單量在500萬隻左右，比去年增長15%。”目前，該公司產品遠銷美國、西班牙、土耳其、巴西等20個國家和地區。這些看似不起眼的工業“小零件”，正在補齊短板，悄然改寫全球產業鏈的供需格局。  資料顯示，截至2026年上半年，國家級專精特新 “小巨人” 超1.76萬家；國家級製造業單項冠軍企業數量超過1800家，主要覆蓋高階裝備、新材料、新一代資訊科技等領域。此外專精特新企業的資本化提速也十分明顯。截至今年7月，國家級專精特新“小巨人”企業的上市數量，佔年內新上市公司數量的比重超過70%。（央視財經）

【北京力爭到2030年農業科技進步貢獻率達80%】記者在24日召開的首都開局起步“十五五”系列主題新聞釋出會上獲悉，北京在“十五五”期間，將立足北京（京津冀）國際科技創新中心定位，統籌推進農業中關村、“種業之都”建設兩大戰略目標，持續推動農業科技創新與產業創新深度融合，力爭到2030年，農業科技進步貢獻率達80%。

據瞭解，“十四五”期間，北京農業科技創新持續突破，全市農業科技進步貢獻率達75%，先後圍繞農業科技頂層設計、龍頭企業培育、科研成果轉化等方面出臺一系列政策措施，目前已形成以北京平谷區為核心，7個輻射區功能錯位、協同發展的創新格局。

一是高標準建設農業中關村，構建高效協同發展新格局，明晰各區功能定位，深化“1+N”協同佈局，打造高能級農業科技創新平臺，加快推進國家農業科技創新港等重大專案建設；二是高水平打造“種業之都”，築牢農業“晶片”，緊扣智慧育種發展主線，打造智慧育種創新平臺，持續培育種業發展增長極；三是攻堅前沿核心技術，聚焦生物育種、“人工智慧+”農業、農業生物製造三大領域，實施30至40項關鍵技術攻關，突破基因編輯、人工智慧設計育種等前沿技術，培育自主原創品種，力爭形成5至10項國際領先成果；四是加速成果落地，圍繞“京品”農業建設都市農業特色品牌，實施100至150項農業科技“小切口”試驗示範專案，最佳化現代農業產業技術體系，以科技創新驅動都市農業提質升級。（新華社）

EU Natural Gas Prices Rise to 3-1/2-Year High — European natural gas prices climbed above €63 per MWh on Friday, reaching their highest level since January 2023, as escalating tensions between the US and Iran raised fresh concerns over Europe's LNG supply security. The US carried out its 13th consecutive night of strikes on Iran, while President Donald Trump warned Tehran and its Houthi allies in Yemen of "major military punishment" if further attacks on vessels in the Red Sea take place. European gas prices have surged more than 10% this week and over 45% since the start of July, as reduced LNG flows from the Persian Gulf intensified competition with Asian buyers for available cargoes. At the same time, unusually hot weather across Europe has lifted electricity demand for cooling, increasing gas consumption. US Natgas Prices Rebound — US natural gas futures climbed above $2.95 per MMBtu, extending their rebound from a two-month low as forecasts for hotter weather boosted expectations of stronger cooling demand. Meteorologists expect temperatures to remain mostly above normal through August 7, likely increasing natural gas consumption by power generators as air conditioning use rises. However, gains were capped by ample supply conditions. EIA data showed gas inventories were 6.4% above the five-year seasonal average as of July 17, while production in the Lower 48 states increased to an average of 110.4 bcfd so far in July, up from 110.0 bcfd in June. LNG export demand also eased, with gas flows to major export terminals averaging 17.4 bcfd so far this month, slightly below June's pace, partly due to scheduled maintenance at Freeport LNG's Texas facility. Meanwhile, Tropical Storm Bertha is being closely monitored as it could disrupt LNG export operations along the US Gulf Coast.

US Building Permits Fall Less than Initially Thought in June — US building permits fell 2.6% month over month to a seasonally adjusted annual rate of 1.374 million in June 2026, according to final data, slightly above the preliminary estimate of 1.367 million. Despite the upward revision, permit issuance remained at a three-month low, highlighting persistent weakness in the housing market as elevated mortgage rates and a growing inventory of unsold new homes continue to weigh on construction activity. Permits for multifamily buildings with five or more units declined 3.1% to 502,000, while single-family permits dropped 2.2% to 872,000, their lowest level in ten months. Regionally, permitting activity decreased in the South (-7.1% to 708,000) and the West (-4.3% to 308,000), while the Midwest (5.2% to 203,000) and the Northeast (16.5% to 155,000) recorded gains.

### 2026-07-24 20:41:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 2

Canada Producer Prices Drop Most in Over 2 Years — The industrial producer price index in Canada fell by 1.4% from the previous month in June of 2026, the sharpest decline since December of 2023, to erase part of the surge in producer prices since January. The brief restoration of energy supply from the Middle East after the memorandum of understanding between Iran and the US drove fuel prices to slide in the month. The energy and petroleum sector of the CPI slid by 9.1%. Meanwhile, prices also fell for non-ferrous metal products (-4.7%) and meat, fish, and dairy products (-0.2%). The Canadian IPPI surged by 13.6% annually, the highest since 2022, as fuel prices remained elevated despite the monthly pullback. Energy and petroleum prices were 36.8% higher, while those for chemical products were 18% up.

### 2026-07-24 20:39:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Wholesale Prices #Importance 1

Canada Raw Materials Prices Plunge 6.9% — Canada’s raw materials prices decreased 6.9% mom in June 2026, the largest monthly decline for the index since July 2022 and the first since August 2025, compared to forecasts of a 1.5% fall. The largest downward contribution came from prices for crude energy products (-13.7%) which declined for the second consecutive month. Both conventional crude oil (-15.7%) and synthetic crude oil (-9.8%) posted monthly decreases as a tentative deal between the US and Iran was announced. Excluding energy crude, the raw materials price index decreased 2.7%. Meanwhile, metal ores, concentrates and scrap (-5.1%) posted its largest monthly decline since June 2023. The driver of the decrease was the gold, silver, and platinum group metal ores and concentrates product group (-8.4%), which registered its largest drop since July 2017. Lower prices for nickel ores and concentrates (-4.3%) made an impact as well. Year-on-year, prices for raw materials increased 20.7%, slowing from a 33.4% gain in May.

【菲爾茲獎得主雅各布·齊默曼轉向AI安全領域，將加入OpenAI】當地時間7月23日，新晉菲爾茲獎得主雅各布·齊默曼（Jacob Tsimerman）宣佈轉向人工智慧安全研究，並將加入OpenAI。OpenAI多名研究負責人已公開歡迎其加入。齊默曼對AI風險的關注並非近期出現。2025年，他與安德魯·克里奇（Andrew Critch）共同發表論文，系統梳理了人工智慧可能導致人類接近滅絕的潛在路徑。論文並未認為AI導致人類滅絕是必然結果，而是希望推動相關預防措施。由於認為AI可能在未來兩年內超越數學家進行數學研究，齊默曼已停止招收新的數學博士生。他表示，不希望培養學生進入一個可能很快發生重大變化甚至消失的職業領域。

### 2026-07-24 20:16:27  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mexico #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Mexico Unemployment Rate Hits Nine-Month High — Mexico's unemployment rate rose to 2.9% in June 2026 from 2.8% in May, the highest level since September 2025 and slightly above market expectations of 2.8%. The number of unemployed people totaled 1.8 million. The unemployment rate stood at 3.0% for women and 2.8% for men. By age group, individuals aged 25 to 44 accounted for 48.4% of the unemployed, followed by those aged 15 to 24 at 29.6%. Among the unemployed, 89.4% had completed at least secondary education. Regarding unemployment duration, 43.5% had been jobless for one month or less, while 30.3% had been unemployed for more than one month but no longer than three months. Meanwhile, the underemployment rate, which measures employed workers seeking additional hours, was 6.6%. The economically inactive population totaled 43.3 million people, or 41.2% of those aged 15 and over.

### 2026-07-24 20:08:39  #國際

【日本自民黨再曝醜聞 多人稱被迫“進貢”大額資金】日本執政黨自由民主黨近日再曝金錢醜聞。該黨位於福岡縣的分支機構被指要求出任該縣議長和副議長的成員“進貢”大額資金，其中一名前議長自曝被索要2000萬日元（約合83萬元人民幣）。 據日本《讀賣新聞》、朝日電視臺等多家媒體24日報道，福岡縣前議長吉松源昭向媒體揭發這一醜聞。吉松源昭自述，他2020年6月出任縣議長前，其所屬的自民黨福岡縣議員團以各種名義向其索要金錢，包括黨派社交活動的打高爾夫球費用、住宿費、交通費和高階餐廳餐飲費等，共計約2000萬日元。他還公開了福岡縣議員團重要人物中尾正幸向其索要現金的錄音。中尾正幸已於24日宣佈辭去縣議員職務，但否認牽涉金錢醜聞。 吉松源昭已退出自民黨。按他的說法，自民黨福岡縣議員團不僅向多人索要資金，還大肆揮霍稅款用於海外考察。他決定站出來發聲，以求改變這一狀況。 除吉松源昭外，另有多人證實曾向自民黨福岡縣議員團“進貢”。福岡縣前副議長江藤秀之表示，他上任前曾“按慣例”向該議員團上交約800萬日元（約合33萬元人民幣）。另有兩人自述，在出任該縣議長和副議長前曾上交數百萬日元。 上述報道引發日本輿論廣泛關注。有網友在日本雅虎新聞網站留言寫道，自民黨在“黑金”醜聞後再次曝出類似事件，暴露出這一政黨的“骯髒本質”，必須徹查此事。 2023年底，自民黨被曝出“黑金”醜聞，部分派閥要求所屬議員按指標出售政治籌款派對券，將超額部分以“回扣”形式返還給議員，而相關資金並未登記在政治資金收支報告中，成為不受監管的秘密資金。（新華社）

Regional partners, citing repeated attacks on targets in Kuwait and Bahrain, are urging a halt to the fighting.

Oil and product prices eased but remained sharply higher on the week as escalating strikes between Iran and the US halted tanker flows through the Persian Gulf and Red Sea. 【東方金誠首席宏觀分析師王青：中國人民銀行宣佈將開展4次隔夜逆回購操作，旨在發揮隔夜逆回購對超短期流動性的調節作用】中國人民銀行7月24日釋出公告稱，為更好匹配銀行體系短期流動性需求，中國人民銀行將在7月29日至7月31日、8月3日開展隔夜逆回購操作，採用固定利率、數量招標。7月29日至7月31日每日開展6000億元，8月3日開展3000億元。東方金誠首席宏觀分析師王青表示，中國人民銀行宣佈將開展4次隔夜逆回購操作，旨在發揮隔夜逆回購對超短期流動性的調節作用，更好匹配金融機構在月末等時點的短期流動性需求。6月底，央行在公開市場操作中增加了隔夜逆回購操作品種，旨在進一步完善利率調控機制、豐富工具期限品種，並於6月29日和30日兩天分別操作3000億元和6000億元。據悉，隔夜逆回購操作在一些特殊時點可以起到“削峰填谷”的作用，開展隔夜逆回購操作，可以提高流動性管理效率、降低金融機構成本。（新華社）

會議指出，習近平總書記在2026世界人工智慧大會暨人工智慧全球治理高階別會議開幕式上的重要講話，全面系統闡述中國對人工智慧全球治理的立場主張，為各方一道把握和應對人工智慧發展的機遇和挑戰，攜手共創人類社會更加美好的未來提出中國方案、貢獻中國智慧，具有重大現實意義和深遠歷史意義。要把總書記這次重要講話同總書記關於人工智慧的系列重要論述結合起來，一體學習領會、整體貫徹落實，全力推動我省人工智慧發展和治理，為廣東現代化建設搶抓機遇、提供支撐。要堅持自立自強抓創新，聚焦基礎硬體、基礎軟體、基礎演算法等關鍵領域、關鍵環節，加強基礎研究和技術攻關，把打造垂類大模型作為重點和特色創新方向，加大對電子資訊、智慧家電、汽車製造、生物醫藥等優勢產業的專用模型開發力度，深入實施“百萬英才匯南粵”行動計劃，精準做好相關領域育才引才工作，推動全省人工智慧科研硬核實力實現整體提升。要突出應用導向，更好賦能千行百業，深入實施“人工智慧+”行動，加快場景培育開放和大規模應用，有力牽引技術突破、促進產品迭代，支援鏈主企業整合上下游，培育壯大產業生態，多措並舉加速人工智慧全域全時全行業高水平應用。要深化開放合作，主動融入全球，突出做好粵港澳合作，用好世界人工智慧大會、APEC峰會等平臺，加快吸聚國內國際企業、技術、人才等要素資源，推動人工智慧產品和服務出海，積極開展“來數加工”、推動“詞元出海”，不斷拓展新興市場，更好幫助全球南方國家加強技術能力建設，努力為構建公正合理的全球人工智慧治理體系作出更大貢獻。要強化風險意識，實現安全發展，著眼防範人工智慧引發的各類內生和衍生風險，加緊制定完善法規制度、應用規範、倫理準則，構建技術檢測、風險預警、應急響應體系，確保人工智慧安全、可靠、可控。（南方+）

### 2026-07-24 19:47:22  #公司 #市場 #國際

【伊朗戰事擾亂供應鏈 印度健怡可樂漲價換包裝】健怡可樂一向在印度市場熱銷，賣得最多的是300毫升鋁罐裝。然而，伊朗戰事延宕擾亂鋁罐供應鏈，可口可樂公司不得不調整進口源，推出330毫升鋁罐裝，並上調售價。媒體認為，這一案例表明，受伊朗戰事影響，不少企業正被迫調整供應鏈以及在主要消費市場提高售價。路透社24日以兩名知情人士為訊息源報道，由於300毫升容量的鋁罐供應緊張，可口可樂公司被迫從東南亞採購成本更高的330毫升鋁罐。原先，印度市場最熱銷的300毫升健怡可樂售價為40印度盧比（約合2.8元人民幣），而近期上架的330毫升罐裝產品售價50印度盧比（3.5元人民幣）。按單位容量計算，價格上漲約13.6%。健怡可樂在印度市場上主要以鋁罐包裝形式銷售，霍爾木茲海峽則是鋁罐及相關原材料運往印度的重要通道。本月早些時候，美國與伊朗戰火重燃，霍爾木茲海峽商業航運再次嚴重受阻，市場擔憂戰事還可能波及其他海上運輸路線。可口可樂公司在印度銷售的多數飲料採用塑膠瓶、玻璃瓶和鋁罐等包裝。無糖可樂“零度可樂”同時使用塑膠瓶和鋁罐包裝，目前供應未受明顯影響。（新華社）

【麥科奧特：MT200605用於急性缺血性腦卒中的II期臨床試驗完成資料庫鎖定】麥科奧特公告，集團自主研發的用於治療急性缺血性腦卒中的候選藥物MT200605的II期臨床試驗已完成資料庫鎖定。MT200605為一種具有雙重協同作用機制的注射用神經保護劑。其設計旨在透過同時啟用TrkB受體和清除氧自由基，一方面抑制神經元凋亡、降低興奮性毒性及減輕炎性反應，從而減少細胞死亡；另一方面透過上游重新啟用神經元細胞的內源性修復程式，改善突觸可塑性並促進神經元再生。

【中企新工廠落地巴西助力智慧配網建設】由中企奧克斯集團投資建設的南森變壓器工廠22日在巴西東南部米納斯吉拉斯州貝廷市舉行投產儀式，該專案將為當地電力行業提供智慧電力裝備解決方案。中國駐里約總領館總領事田敏在影片致辭中表示，當前，巴西政府正大力推行“再工業化”戰略，米納斯吉拉斯州致力於加強基礎設施建設，推動能源轉型，中巴地方合作優勢互補，前景廣闊。米納斯吉拉斯州經濟發展廳副廳長丹尼爾·梅德拉多致辭說，來自中國的投資將助力當地可持續清潔能源發展，這種互利共贏的合作“讓巴西人感到自豪”。巴西南森執行長範翀介紹，新工廠採用智慧製造理念建設，引入多項先進工藝和智慧測試平臺，實現生產全過程數字化管理。工廠將重點佈局配電自動化裝置、新能源配套產品等領域，為巴西能源轉型提供完整的智慧電力裝備解決方案。據悉，新工廠投產後年產能可達14.4萬臺變壓器，有望創造約300個就業崗位。（新華社）

### 2026-07-24 19:13:36  #MKT News #Market Themes #AI Revolution #CNH #Impact bullish #CSI300 #Hang Seng Index

【UBS: China's leading model training costs about one-tenth of overseas peers】UBS China internet analyst Xiong Wei said on July 24 that demand stratification is creating divergent pricing power across model tiers. UBS estimates China’s top models have training costs roughly one-tenth of overseas leaders; inference API pricing for Chinese models is about 10–20% of comparable foreign models while still delivering 20–40% gross margins—slightly below U.S. peers but economically healthy. The firm says the advantage is structural, not subsidy-driven, reflecting training algorithm and architecture innovations, different model strategy priorities, and open-source ecosystem synergies.

Swiss 10-Year Yield Eases from 2-Month High — Switzerland's 10-year government bond yield fell below 0.50%, retreating from a more than two-month high as investors assessed tariffs disputes amid escalating geopolitical tensions. Oil prices continue to surge as the US-Iran conflict intensified, increasing the risk of energy supply disruptions at another key maritime chokepoint and fueling concerns over higher inflationary pressures, and slower economic growth. At the same time, the Trump administration imposed new tariffs on Swiss imports, while remaining within the previously established tariff ceilings of up to 12.5%. The Swiss National Bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 0% in June and reiterated its readiness to intervene in the foreign exchange market to prevent excessive franc appreciation. Meeting minutes also showed policymakers acknowledged higher near-term inflation risks, although the medium-term inflation outlook remained broadly unchanged.

【Zhejiang backs listings, M&A and expanded tech-innovation bond issuance for sci‑tech firms】Zhejiang provincial leadership issued guidance to build a top-tier innovation ecosystem and more competitive business environment, prioritizing high-quality tech-finance development. Measures include accelerating a tech-finance system aligned with innovation, strengthening full life‑cycle financing for sci‑tech firms, and promoting an innovation scoring system to steer financial resources toward these companies. Launch a 'patient capital' initiative, marketize operation of social‑security sci‑tech funds, and boost the leverage role of industrial funds to channel capital earlier, in smaller tickets, for longer tenors and into hard‑tech. Deepen the Phoenix Action to support sci‑tech firm listings and M&A. Use re‑lending and other monetary policy tools, establish dedicated bank sci‑tech lending mechanisms, expand tech‑innovation bond issuance, and improve tech‑finance risk‑sharing. Targets: by 2027 tech loans should keep steady growth and tech insurance coverage exceed 700 billion yuan; by 2030 Zhejiang aims to rank among the country’s leaders in tech‑innovation bond issuance.

Bank of Russia Cuts Interest Rate as Expected — The Bank of Russia cut its policy rate by 25bps to 14.0% in its July 2026 decision, as expected by most of financial markets, to extend its cutting cycle since the rate was at a record high of 21% up to June of last year. The central bank noted that monetary conditions remained moderately tight despite the elevated level of real rates. The economy grew only at a moderate pace so far in 2026 as robust consumer demand was offset by declining capacity in key sectors, partially due to fuel shortages as Ukrainian forces struck major refineries across Russia. Consequently, the latest data showed the first annual contraction in the GDP in three years in Q1. Meanwhile, the CBR noted that underlying inflation was steady at levels close to the 4% mark. The surge in fuel costs were deemed temporary and one-off by the CBR, although policymakers remained attentive to growing inflation expectations due to the growing budget deficit by the government.

Crude Oil Set for 10% Weekly Gain — Crude oil slipped toward $89 per barrel on Friday but remained up by around 10% for the week, as traders continued to assess mounting geopolitical risks to global energy supplies. The US launched a 13th straight day of strikes on Iran, with both sides ruling out near-term talks. President Trump also threatened "major military punishment" against Iran and the Houthis over any further attacks on Red Sea shipping and said he was considering a "massive attack" on Iran. His remarks followed attacks by Iran-backed Houthi militants on two Saudi oil tankers in the Red Sea, a key alternative export route for Saudi Arabia as fighting continued to disrupt traffic through the Strait of Hormuz. Asian buyers have begun discussing rerouting Saudi crude shipments through the Suez Canal and around Africa. Adding to supply constraints, the Caspian Pipeline Consortium suspended crude loadings at its Black Sea terminal after tanker attacks, disrupting around 80% of Kazakhstan's oil exports.

【農業農村部：目前秋糧形勢總體不錯】農業農村部副部長張興旺24日說，當前秋糧形勢總體不錯。農業農村部將堅持全過程全環節不放鬆，實施奮戰100天強田管抗災害奪秋糧豐收行動，全力奪取秋糧和全年糧食豐收。張興旺在當天舉行的國新辦釋出會上說，夏糧生產克服大面積晚播、收穫期多輪降雨等影響，產量首次突破3000億斤，實現全年糧食生產首戰告捷；夏收油料也再獲豐收。秋糧是全年糧食生產的大頭，約佔全年糧食產量的四分之三。“當前秋糧形勢總體不錯，體現在播種面積和苗情長勢兩個方面。”他表示，除南方部分雙季晚稻還在栽插外，秋糧種植面積基本落地，預計穩中略增，而且基本種在了豐產期。各地深入推進大面積單產提升，整合推廣水肥一體化、“一噴多促”等措施，秋糧苗情長勢總體比較正常。（新華社）

### 2026-07-24 18:13:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #Luxembourg #Balance of Trade #Importance 1

Luxembourg Trade Deficit Narrows in May — Luxemburg's trade deficit narrowed to €0.66 billion in May 2026 from €0.67 in the same month a year earlier. Exports rose 6.4% year-on-year to €1.33 billion, supported by higher shipments of chemical and other related products (27.9%), base metal manufactured goods (8.5%) and transport materials (7.7%). Shipments to the United States (8.1%), Germany (5.9%) and Belgium (5.9%) saw the highest gains. Imports increased at a slower pace of 3.8% to €1.99 billion, driven by stronger purchases of mineral fuels and lubricants (52.1%), chemical and related products (22.3%), and machinery and equipment (7.4%). Purchases from the United States surged 153.0%, while purchases from Japan increased 74.8%.

【HKEX publishes consultation summary on proposals to boost listing competitiveness】HKEX published a consultation-summary, receiving 73 responses. All previously proposed measures were broadly supported and will be adopted with minor revisions. For different voting-rights/dual-class share structures, market-cap thresholds are cut to ≥HK$20 bln, or ≥HK$6 bln with audited revenue ≥HK$600 mln in the most recent fiscal year. The confidential (non-public) listing-application route will be extended to allow all new applicants to submit applications confidentially.

### 2026-07-24 18:01:32  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macau #Tourist Arrivals #Importance 1

Macau Tourist Arrivals Drop 3.1% in June — The number of tourist arrivals in Macau fell 3.1% year-on-year to 2.801 million in June 2026, reversing a 3.4% increase in the previous month. This marked the first contraction since February 2025, as overnight visitors dropped 9.0% to 1.164 million, while same-day visitors increased 1.6% to 1.637 million. Additionally, the average length of stay shortened by 0.1 day from a year earlier to 1.1 days, while the average stay of overnight visitors remained unchanged at 2.4 days. By source market, arrivals from China declined 2.3% year-on-year to 1.950 million, although visitors traveling under the Individual Visit Scheme rose 2.6% to 1.054 million. Arrivals from Hong Kong (577,275) and the Philippines (36,686) also fell by 5.9% and 22.0%, respectively. In contrast, visitor arrivals from Taiwan (95,277), Thailand (13,228), and India (12,735) increased by 12.0%, 27.5%, and 6.9%, respectively. In the first half of 2026, total visitor arrivals rose 9.0% year-on-year to 20.944 million.

### 2026-07-24 17:53:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Steel Holds Decline from 1-Month High — Steel rebar futures in China were close to CNY 3,060 per tonne, holding the pullback from one-month high of CNY 3,130 on July 16th amid low demand in China. The oversupply of housing that caused their prolonged property crisis limited investment for new construction. New home prices contracted further in June, and construction starts by floor area fell 23.4% annually in the first half of the year. Construction accounts for one-third of Chinese steel demand, making up for most of rebar purchases. Manufacturing growth was also muted as low consumer demand was combined with protectionist policies limiting the magnitude of foreign orders for goods producers, especially due to the softening momentum for auto production growth. Consequently, domestic steel output totaled 500 million tonnes in the first half of the year, around 3% below levels from 2025. Still, ample infrastructure expenditure from Beijing, including for power plants, grids, and electricity storage, supported consumption.

【Sources say Bank of Japan plans to keep policy rate at 1% next week】Nikkei reports the Bank of Japan intends to hold its policy rate at 1% at the July 30-31 meeting; a majority of the nine-member policy board is expected to vote to maintain rates. Board members broadly view financial conditions as accommodative and acknowledge a case for further rate hikes to stabilise inflation, but many say there is no urgency to act. Some members had pushed for a follow-up increase in July after June’s hike; the BOJ is closely monitoring the effects of June’s move amid Middle East tensions and oil-price inflation risk. The committee may raise its fiscal 2026 median real GDP growth forecast from April’s 0.5% as economic conditions have improved — alternative Middle East oil supplies have increased, corporate output concerns have eased, and AI-related sectors have outperformed expectations, supporting growth.

【周鴻禕談Open AI智慧體逃逸事故：智慧體安全比模型安全嚴重十倍】近期，OpenAI智慧體在內部安全測試中突破隔離環境、跨網入侵HuggingFace生產系統的事件持續發酵。360集團創始人周鴻禕將這起事件定義為"AI安全發展的關鍵分水嶺"，並圍繞智慧體失控風險與行業治理路徑發表觀點，引發業內廣泛討論。針對輿論普遍將本次AI逃逸等同於科幻敘事裡“AI 覺醒反叛”的認知誤區，周鴻禕作出糾正，本次事故並非AI產生自我意識，而是典型的目標驅動型失控，無主觀惡意卻行為不可預判，具備更為緊迫的現實安全威脅。他特別強調，智慧體具備實操能力，如果行業在高速迭代技術的同時持續放寬安全約束，類似的智慧體失控、自主攻擊事件只會更加頻繁。對此，周鴻禕從事前管控、實時監管、源頭防禦、風險預警、攻防體系、產業發展六個維度，梳理了智慧體安全治理思路。（新浪科技）

【上海清算所與上海資產管理協會聯合舉辦資管機構銀行間市場業務交流會】為推動更多資管機構積極參與銀行間債券和衍生品中央對手清算業務，近期，上海清算所與上海資產管理協會聯合舉辦“資管機構銀行間市場業務交流會”。本次會議旨在加強金融基礎設施與行業協會協同合作，充分發揮中央對手清算優勢，在有效防範風險的基礎上，支援資產管理行業更好配置債券資產並應用衍生品提升風險管理能力，共同建設一個有深度、有廣度、更具流動性和韌性的銀行間債券與衍生品市場，服務上海打造金融開放樞紐門戶。下一步，上海清算所將繼續在人民銀行和上海市指導下，立足監管所期、市場所需、自身所長，推動參與者隊伍提質擴容，支援銀行理財、保險資管、公募基金等多類資管類機構充分運用簡單透明衍生品管理風險，並持續提供優質高效的債券服務、專業的估值定價與指數編制服務，促進銀行間債券和衍生品市場穩健有序發展，助力上海國際金融中心建設。

【全國幫扶主導產業總產值突破2萬億元】農業農村部幫扶司司長許健民24日在國新辦舉行的新聞釋出會上表示，今年以來我國產業幫扶持續加力，各地以縣為單位因地制宜確定幫扶主導產業，全國幫扶主導產業總產值突破2萬億元。 今年我國進入常態化幫扶的新階段。各地區各部門各司其職、各盡其能，常態化精準幫扶各項工作有序推進。 具體來看，許健民介紹，監測幫扶機制有效執行，各地採取農戶自主申報、部門大資料預警、基層日常排查等多種方式，及時識別防止返貧致貧物件，因人因戶精準落實幫扶措施；就業幫扶持續深化，截至今年6月份，脫貧人口務工規模已經超過了3200萬人；重點區域穩步發展，在西部地區，確定了160個國家鄉村振興重點幫扶縣，集中資源支援實施一批補短板促發展惠民生的專案。 許健民說，下一步，農業農村部將健全建強監測體系，監測範圍逐步拓展到全體農村人口，實現全覆蓋；強化開發式幫扶舉措，推動實施幫扶產業全鏈開發惠農增收工程，持續發揮產業聯農帶農作用；加強重點區域傾斜幫扶，支援欠發達地區提升發展能力。保持責任不松，力度不減，真抓實幹，全力抓好抓緊常態化幫扶。(新華社)

【新科菲爾茲獎得主鄧煜：為中國數學進步感到鼓舞】2026年菲爾茲獎得主、中國籍數學家鄧煜23日在接受新華社記者採訪時表示，近年來中國在數學及基礎科學研究方面取得重要進展，他為此感到高興和鼓舞。他同時指出，國際交流與合作對於推動數學研究發展越來越重要。鄧煜與合作者一起解決了狹義希爾伯特第六問題，首次以數學嚴格方式推匯出從微觀粒子系統到宏觀玻爾茲曼方程的完整邏輯鏈條。他表示，獲得菲爾茲獎是對自己的科研工作以及所從事研究領域的重要認可。當前數學領域有許多令人振奮的新進展，比如偏微分方程領域中的計算機輔助證明，以及統計物理領域一些關鍵問題取得的新突破，這些都是數學研究的重要前沿方向。（新華社）

### 2026-07-24 17:25:47  #其他

【國家金融監督管理總局就《國家金融監督管理總局行政複議辦法（徵求意見稿）》公開徵求意見】為深入貫徹《中華人民共和國行政複議法》及其實施條例，規範金融監管領域行政複議工作，推進法治政府建設，金融監管總局起草了《國家金融監督管理總局行政複議辦法（徵求意見稿）》（以下簡稱《辦法》），現向社會公開徵求意見。《辦法》共八章五十六條，主要內容有：一是確立行政複議工作原則。規定總局系統行政複議工作堅持黨的領導，遵循合法、公正、公開、高效、便民、為民原則，發揮行政複議化解行政爭議的主渠道作用。二是界定行政複議範圍和管轄。根據行政複議法及其實施條例，明確行政複議範圍和排除情形。以層級管轄、逐級審理為原則確定有管轄權的行政複議機關。三是明確行政複議申請和受理程式。細化行政複議參加人、申請方式和申請書要素等，規定行政複議前置情形。明確受理標準和時限，對合並審理、重複複議等作出規定。四是規範行政複議審理和決定事項。規定調查取證、聽取意見、組織聽證、查閱複製卷宗等審理程式，保障當事人程式性權利。細化規範性檔案附帶審查制度，明確審理期限、行政複議決定型別及作出依據。歡迎社會各界提出寶貴意見。金融監管總局將認真研究各方反饋意見，進一步修改完善《辦法》並適時釋出實施，依法保護公民、法人和其他組織的合法權益，更好發揮行政複議制度效能，為建設高水平金融法治提供有力支撐。

【日本生成式AI使用率提升但仍不足60%】日本總務省24日釋出《2026年版資訊通訊白皮書》。白皮書依託2025年度調研資料指出，日本個人群體生成式人工智慧（AI）使用率較2024年度大幅上漲，但整體佔比仍未突破60%。資料顯示，日本個人生成式AI使用率為58.8%，美國、德國兩國使用率持平，均達75.6%。使用頻次維度上，日本僅有29.9%的使用者幾乎每日使用生成式AI，該佔比同樣低於美、德兩國。從使用場景來看，日本民眾主要使用美國開放人工智慧研究中心（OpenAI）研發的ChatGPT等文字生成類AI工具，多用於辦公事務與資訊檢索；語音生成、音樂創作、程式碼生成等其他型別生成式AI服務，使用者滲透率均不足10%。（新華財經）

Euro Weighed by Dollar Strength and Trade Risks — The euro traded below $1.14, near its one-year low reached in June, as investors assessed the ECB's policy outlook and fresh PMI data, while the dollar stayed supported by Middle East risks and new US tariffs. The ECB kept interest rates unchanged as expected but signaled that a September rate hike is becoming increasingly likely. Rising energy prices, with oil nearing $100 per barrel and natural gas prices surging amid the US-Iran conflict, have reinforced inflation concerns and expectations for further tightening after the summer. Several policymakers, including Bundesbank President Joachim Nagel, also warned that inflation risks remain elevated and further rate increases may be needed. Meanwhile, the S&P Global survey showed Eurozone business activity returned to growth in July, beating forecasts. The US also imposed new tariffs on imports from 60 trading partners, including the EU, with the European Commission saying the measures were broadly in line with the EU-US trade agreement.

### 2026-07-24 17:01:44  #市場

【深交所修訂釋出ABS稽核重點關注事項指引 持續夯實市場發展制度基礎】7月24日，深交所釋出《深圳證券交易所資產支援證券掛牌條件稽核業務指引第2號——稽核重點關注事項（2026年修訂）》，聚焦不動產領域存量資產盤活痛點難點，健全資產支援證券規則體系，從源頭築牢資產質量防線，促進市場規範健康發展。本次修訂結合最新行業監管政策和業務實踐，對指引進行梳理完善，補齊制度短板。一是新設不動產基礎資產專章，推動產品規範發展。明確不動產專案合規准入標準，強化專案運營與現金流要求，規範資產評估和現金流預測，壓實相關機構運營管理職責。二是新增對具有權益屬性特徵的不動產資產支援證券專項要求，夯實產品權益屬性。規範機構間REITs產品機制設計，在不動產基礎資產通用准入要求基礎上，進一步壓實資產估值，強化運營管理要求。三是完善債權類基礎資產關注要點，最佳化未來經營收入類專案成本覆蓋要求，統一不動產抵押貸款專案關於不動產資產的稽核要求，實現各類資產監管標準統一銜接、精準適配。

【為什麼分步推進揮發性有機物環境保護稅試點工作？三部門回應】《徵收揮發性有機物環境保護稅試點實施辦法》近日印發。為什麼要分步推進揮發性有機物環境保護稅試點工作？財政部、稅務總局、生態環境部有關負責人表示，揮發性有機物排放源項多，涉及的工藝環節也多，排放量計算較為複雜。為平穩推進試點工作，我們結合不同行業企業排放規模、環保監管要求、徵管條件等情況，分兩步推開試點，先在“印刷”、“化學原料和化學制品製造”、“石油、煤炭及其他燃料加工”、“醫藥製造”、“鍊鐵、鍊鋼”、“通用裝置製造”、“專用裝置製造”、“汽車製造”等八個行業開展試點，這八個行業揮發性有機物排放量佔工業源總排量的70%以上。下一步，財政部、稅務總局、生態環境部將密切跟蹤試點情況，及時總結經驗，評估試點效果，適時在其他行業全面推開徵收揮發性有機物環境保護稅試點工作。

UK Services Activity Returns to Growth in July — The S&P Global UK Services PMI rose to 51.8 in July 2026 from 48.8 in June, reaching a three-month high and signaling a return to expansion in the services sector, beating market expectations of 49.4, flash data showed. Business activity increased for the first time since April, with firms reporting stronger demand for consumer services, particularly in parts of the hospitality industry, supported by the FIFA World Cup. However, many companies continued to highlight elevated client uncertainty linked to the conflict in the Middle East, while some also said unusually hot weather weighed on activity. Outstanding business declined further, reflecting another marked reduction in backlogs of work, and service providers continued to trim staffing levels. On the inflation front, input cost pressures moderated, with service firms reporting softer increases in operating expenses. Business confidence also improved, as companies became more optimistic about activity over the coming year.

The survey, used in ECB policy deliberations, forecasts average inf this year at 2.7% (unchanged), raises the 2027 forecast to 2.2% from 2.1%, and projects 2.0% for 2028. ### 2026-07-24 16:51:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

UK Factory Conditions Improve in July — The S&P Global Flash UK Manufacturing PMI increased to 52.8 in July 2026 from 52.5 in June, beating forecasts of 52. Goods producers recorded an expansion of production levels for the fourth month in a row in July. The rate of growth accelerated to its strongest since September 2024, attributed to generally improving market conditions and a subsequent strengthening of new order intakes. Also, manufacturers indicated a rise in unfinished work for the first time since April 2022 and there was a marginal increase in staffing levels. At the same time, softer cost pressures were observed. Some manufacturers noted instances of lower raw material prices and oil-related surcharges (especially for plastics). Manufacturers indicated the least marked downturn in supplier performance since February, despite many firms noting longer international shipping times.

### 2026-07-24 16:49:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Composite PMI #Importance 1

UK Private Sector Activity Recovers — The S&P Global UK Composite PMI rose to 52.1 in July of 2026 from 49.3 in the previous month, rebounding from two months of contraction and well ahead market expectations of a 49.7, according to a flash estimate. The improved activity levels were supported by both manufacturing (53.6 vs 52.6 in June) and services (51.8 vs 48.8), with the latter recovering from declines in the second quarter after the war in Iran triggered a surge in energy prices, lifting operation costs and squeezing demand from consumers. The services sector was also boosted by hospitality as good weather and FIFA world cup games drove households to increase consumption. New work received by firm inched higher on the aggregate, with factories having the largest improvement in four years. Still, companies continued to go through backlogs. Meanwhile, the start of the month saw a drop in input costs amid logistics improvements from the Middle East, although high labor costs remained a pressure on headcounts.

### 2026-07-24 16:47:52  #MKT News

【SFC refines regulatory framework for single-day leveraged and inverse products to ensure orderly trading】Hong Kong’s SFC issued a revised circular requiring leveraged and inverse products whose capacity is highly sensitive to market moves to adopt a flexible leverage structure. Under the new framework, leverage may be adjusted daily within existing caps (leveraged products up to 2x; inverse products down to -2x). Product providers may lower target leverage when necessary to create headroom to manage products during periods of heavy trading. The leverage for the next trading day must be disclosed after each market close. Daily potential adjustments will reinforce these products’ single-day nature and remind investors they are not suitable for holding beyond one trading day.

### 2026-07-24 16:46:04  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Services PMI #Importance 1

Euro Area Services Sector Returns to Growth — The S&P Global Eurozone Services PMI rose to 51.6 in July 2026 from 49.4 in June, marking a five-month high and comfortably exceeding market expectations of 49.8, flash data showed. The reading signaled a return to expansion in the services sector after a three-month contraction, supported by a renewed increase in business activity. Employment also improved, with service providers driving overall job creation across the private sector. On the cost front, operating expenses continued to rise at a pace broadly unchanged from the previous month, reflecting persistent input cost pressures. However, output price inflation eased, indicating that firms were passing on costs to customers at a slower rate. Business confidence also strengthened, with sentiment regarding the year-ahead outlook improving across the services sector as companies grew more optimistic about future demand and economic conditions.

Slovenia Tourist Arrivals Up 1.9% in June — Tourist arrivals in Slovenia increased by 1.9% year-on-year to nearly 810 thousand in June 2026, the highest level since August 2025, following a 10.1% rise in the previous month. Foreign tourist arrivals grew by 2.1% to 652.5 thousand, with visitors mainly coming from Czech Republic (14.3%), Poland (15.2%), and the UK (26.1%), while arrivals declined from Germany (-16.9%) and Austria (-9.1%). Domestic tourism rose by 1.1% to 157.5 thousand. Overnight stays went up by 1.5% to around 2 million, with most tourists staying in mountain resorts (5.1%), health resorts (1.7%), and the capital (0.9%). In the first half of the year, total tourist arrivals increased by 5.0% year-on-year to 2.941 million, while overnight stays rose by 5.7% to 7.285 million.

### 2026-07-24 16:34:37  #Trading Economics #Economy #Macau #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Macau Inflation Rate Eases in June — The annual inflation rate in Macau eased to 1.24% in June 2026 from an over two-year high of 1.43% in the previous month. The slowdown was mainly driven by softer inflation in transport (3.71% vs 4.79%), miscellaneous goods and services (2.36% vs 3.56%), housing and fuels (0.39% vs 0.59%), and health (0.87% vs 0.89%). Meanwhile, price growth accelerated for food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.31% vs 1.19%), alcoholic beverages and tobacco (0.09% vs -0.46%), clothing and footwear (3.17% vs 2.91%), household furnishings and services (0.81% vs 0.76%), information and communication (1.17% vs 1.06%), and recreation, sport and culture (1.45% vs 1.24%), while education inflation remained unchanged (1.05% vs 1.05%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices fell 0.15% in June, reversing a 0.17% increase in the previous month and marking the second monthly contraction of 2026, after March's decline.

### 2026-07-24 16:22:50  #Trading Economics #Markets #Germany #Stock Market #Importance 1

DAX Rebounds as SAP Surges — Frankfurt's DAX 40 rose 0.6% to around 24,900 on Friday, rebounding from a near one-month low as investors digested strong corporate earnings and upbeat PMI data despite escalating Middle East tensions and fresh US tariffs. SAP surged 6% after reporting better-than-expected quarterly revenue, supported by robust cloud backlog growth and strong demand for its AI platform, while Atoss Software gained 5% on solid cloud-driven earnings. In contrast, Adidas fell 3% after disappointing investors ahead of next week's earnings release. Automakers underperformed, with Volkswagen slipping 1% after reporting weaker second-quarter earnings and lowering its revenue outlook. Porsche, BMW, and Mercedes-Benz also traded lower. ### 2026-07-24 16:18:18  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro area #Inflation Expectations #Importance 1

Euro Area Consumer Inflation Expectations Fall for 2nd Month — Eurozone median inflation expectations for the next 12 months fell for a second consecutive month to 3% in June 2026, the lowest since February, from 3.5% in May. Consumers expect the price of their home to increase by 3.4% over the next 12 months, compared with 3.6% in May. Expectations for mortgage interest rates went up to 5% from 4.9%. Meanwhile, expectations for inflation three years ahead also declined to 2.8% from 2.9% while expectations for inflation five years ahead remained unchanged at 2.4%. Uncertainty about inflation over the next 12 months decreased for the second consecutive month, but remained higher than the level prevailing before the start of the war in the Middle East. Elsewhere, expected nominal spending growth over the next 12 months decreased to 3.6% from 3.8% in May. Expectations for economic growth over the next 12 months increased to -1.4% from -1.7% and expectations for the unemployment rate 12 months ahead decreased to 11.2% from 11.3% in May.

### 2026-07-24 16:14:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

Eurozone Manufacturing Growth Picks Up Slightly — The S&P Global Eurozone Manufacturing PMI rose to 52.0 in July 2026 from 51.4 in June, above expectations of 51.5, preliminary data showed. This also marked the steepest expansion in three months, supported by a stronger increase in output, as the manufacturing output index climbed to 53.0 from 51.7, marking the strongest pace since March 2022. Manufacturers kept purchasing activity broadly unchanged, while easing supply-chain disruptions allowed firms to increase input inventories for the first time in three-and-a-half years. However, manufacturers continued to reduce staffing levels. On the price front, manufacturing input cost inflation slowed in July, while output price inflation also eased, although cost pressures remained elevated. Looking ahead, manufacturers' confidence regarding future output improved slightly.

【兩部門：由於離岸信託往往設立在資訊透明度低、稅負較低的國家和地區，部分個人利用其轉移資產、隱匿財富及逃避稅】近日，財政部、稅務總局釋出了《關於離岸信託徵收個人所得稅有關事項的公告》。財政部稅政司、稅務總局所得稅司有關負責人就此回答了記者提問。近年來，一些個人透過設立離岸信託進行財富代際傳承、跨境資產配置以及風險管理。由於離岸信託往往設立在資訊透明度低、稅負較低的國家和地區，部分個人利用其轉移資產、隱匿財富及逃避稅。按照我國個人所得稅法對居民全球所得徵稅的基本原則，《公告》明確了離岸信託設立、存續、終止清算等環節的徵稅規定，有利於提高稅收確定性和透明度，便於納稅人合規遵從；有利於促進社會公平，維護國家權益，穩定納稅人預期。

FTSE 100 Trades Higher, Set for Weekly Gain — The FTSE 100 edged higher on Friday, with gains in financial stocks offsetting weakness across the energy, mining, healthcare and consumer sectors. HSBC and Lloyds Banking rose more than 1%, while Barclays, NatWest and Standard Chartered also advanced. Antofagasta traded modestly higher after resuming operations at its Los Pelambres copper mine following a weather-related disruption. Reckitt Benckiser also posted small gains after announcing the sale of its Russian hygiene business to Arnest Management, a deal expected to result in a post-tax loss of about £175 million. Meanwhile, Shell and BP came under pressure as oil prices eased after their recent rally. On the economic front, UK retail sales surprised to the upside, rising 1% in June against expectations of a 0.3% decline. Excluding fuel, sales increased 1.1%, well above forecasts for a 0.5% fall. The FTSE 100 remained on track for a weekly gain of around 0.7%.

### 2026-07-24 16:10:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Composite PMI #Importance 2

Eurozone Private Sector Activity Returns to Growth — The S&P Global Eurozone Composite PMI rose to 51.9 in July 2026 from 50.0 in June, well above market expectations of 50.3, a preliminary estimate showed. The reading signaled the first expansion in business activity in four months, supported by a renewed expansion in services and the fastest increase in manufacturing output since March 2022. Germany grew for the first time in four months, while France's downturn eased to a marginal contraction. Elsewhere, the rest of the eurozone recorded its strongest expansion in eight months. New orders rose at the fastest pace since April 2023, while export orders posted their smallest decline since March 2022. Employment increased for the first time this year, supply-chain pressures eased, allowing manufacturers to rebuild input inventories for the first time in three-and-a-half years, and both input cost and output price inflation moderated. Business confidence also improved to a five-month high, although it remained below pre-conflict levels.

【Euro area business activity returns to expansion in July for first time in four months; new orders drive recovery】Preliminary S&P Global euro area composite PMI rose to 51.9 in July from 50.0 in June, the highest in five months and well above economists’ 50.3 forecast, signalling a return to expansion after four months. New orders expanded for the first time since February, with the pace of growth the strongest since April 2023. Export orders, including intra-euro-area trade, remain in contraction but the decline was the smallest since March 2022. Services PMI climbed to 51.6 from 49.4, ending a three-month contraction and marking a five-month high; manufacturing PMI increased to 52.0 from 51.4, with manufacturing output at its highest growth rate in 52 months and above the 51.5 market expectation. S&P Global chief business economist Chris Williamson said the pickup is welcome but that sustained momentum may be constrained by ongoing geopolitical uncertainty.

### 2026-07-24 16:00:59  #其他

【明年起，企業年金個人賬戶轉移接續可以線上辦理】人力資源社會保障部辦公廳印發了《企業年金個人賬戶轉移接續規程（暫行）》，就企業年金個人賬戶轉移接續給出了具體的管理辦法。從2027年1月1日起，職工可以透過登入國家社會保險公共服務平臺、全國人社政務服務平臺、電子社保卡、掌上12333App等全國統一線上服務渠道，申請轉移接續本人企業年金個人賬戶，並查詢辦理進度。《規程》規定：職工已參加新就業單位企業年金的，選擇名下新就業單位的企業年金個人賬戶作為歸集賬戶。新就業單位沒有建立企業年金或者未參加新就業單位企業年金且需歸集多個企業年金個人賬戶時，區分不同情形，選擇一個企業年金集合計劃管理的保留賬戶或者最後一個原就業單位管理的企業年金賬戶作為歸集賬戶。之後，職工向管理歸集賬戶的企業年金受託人提出轉移接續申請。企業年金受託人（包括法人受託機構和理事會）透過養老保險第二支柱資訊平臺接受轉移接續業務申請及資料互動，辦理完成後向資訊平臺和新就業單位反饋。《規程》提出，退休人員正在領取企業年金待遇的，可以申請將本人其他企業年金個人賬戶資金轉移至正在領取的企業年金個人賬戶。各企業年金受託人、賬戶管理人應當加強資訊化建設，按照本規程要求做好轉移接續工作。（央視新聞）

The zone covers about 7,300 sq km and achieved a major exploration breakthrough in 2021, filling a gap in national demonstration construction for this play. Sinopec said the project provides a replicable, scalable technical pathway for large-scale, higher-efficiency development of similar shale-oil resources. FTSE MIB Gains — The FTSE MIB rose to around 51,600 on Friday, trimming losses of 2.8% in the previous session, as investors remained focused on developments in the Middle East. Brent crude eased from $100 per barrel but stayed at elevated levels. Markets also digested the latest trade actions from President Donald Trump, who introduced tariffs of 10% to 12.5% on imports from most major trading partners after an investigation into alleged shortcomings in preventing forced labor across supply chains, with the tariff rate for the EU expected to remain capped at 10%. Among individual stocks, Intesa Sanpaolo (+1.74%), Banco BPM (+1.56%), and Azimut Holding (+1.25%) led the gains, while Tenaris (-1.33%) and Lottomatica (-1.27%) were among the biggest decliners. Meanwhile, Poste Italiane shares were nearly flat despite the company reporting revenue and adjusted operating profit above expectations. For the week, the index is heading for a third consecutive decline.

【住房城鄉建設部印發通知 進一步加強建築市場監管 持續最佳化建築市場環境】為貫徹落實黨中央、國務院有關決策部署，進一步夯實建築市場經營主體責任，加強建築市場監管，最佳化建築市場環境，規範招標投標市場秩序，加快構建“寬進、嚴管、重處”建築市場監管機制，住房城鄉建設部現就有關工作印發通知。其中提到，完善全國建築市場監管公共服務平臺與省級建築市場監管一體化工作平臺，加快推進與工程建設專案審批管理、工程質量安全監管、建築工人管理、公共資源交易、投資專案審批等管理平臺互聯互通，推進監管資訊共享。推進“人工智慧＋招標投標監管”，積極運用大資料、人工智慧等數字技術，建立貫通專案標前、標中、標後全過程的分析預警模型，加強資料自動分析比對，提高應招未招、圍標串標等違法違規線索發現能力。加快監管數智化轉型，透過監管系統資料比對，對交易專案、各方主體行為等進行深度分析，實施資訊化預警，有效甄別轉包、違法分包、掛靠、掛證等違法違規行為，提升建築市場監管效能。各級住房城鄉建設主管部門要高度重視建築市場監管工作，進一步完善工作機制，創新監管方式，持續最佳化建築市場環境，促進建築業高質量發展。要強化責任追究，依法嚴肅查處建築市場違法違規行為，涉嫌違紀違法犯罪的，及時移送紀檢監察機關、司法機關處理。

### 2026-07-24 15:45:16  #Trading Economics #Economy #Germany #Composite PMI #Importance 1

German Private Sector Activity Returns to Growth — The S&P Global Flash Germany Composite PMI rose to 51.2 in July 2026 from 49.5 in June, beating forecasts of 49.8. It is the highest reading in four months, with the private sector activity moving back to growth after three straight months of contraction, driven by a marked upturn in manufacturing production and a nearly stagnation in services. There was also a renewed increase in new export orders and firms were slightly more optimistic about the year-ahead outlook for activity, which was reflected in a slowdown in the pace of job losses. As for prices, there was a slightly faster rise in input costs, owing to increased pressures facing services firms. Average prices charged for goods and services meanwhile rose at the slowest rate for four months. Despite the July improvement, the path to a sustainable recovery still seems very much uncertain. given the escalating hostilities in the Middle East, which have put renewed upward pressure on global energy prices

German Manufacturing Growth Hits Four-Month High — Germany's S&P Global Manufacturing PMI rose to 52.2 in July 2026 from 50.3 in June, well above market expectations of 50.5, according to a preliminary estimate. The reading signaled the strongest expansion in the sector in four months, driven by a sharp increase in output, which grew at its fastest pace in nearly four-and-a-half years amid stronger order inflows, higher capacity utilization, and frontloading activity. New orders continued to improve, with export demand posting its strongest increase since February 2022. On the inflation front, input cost growth eased to a five-month low, while output price inflation also moderated. Business confidence strengthened during the month but remained below its historical average.

Germany Services Sector Contracts at Softer Pace — Germany's S&P Global Flash Services PMI rose to 49.6 in July 2026 from 48.6 in June, marking a four month high and beating market expectations of 49, according to preliminary estimates. Although the index remained just below the 50 threshold, it pointed to a much slower pace of contraction, with business activity nearing stabilization after four consecutive months of decline. The downturn in new business also eased, reflecting improving demand conditions across the services sector. Business confidence strengthened and climbed back above its long run average, signalling greater optimism about the outlook. On the price front, input cost inflation accelerated slightly, with firms citing higher fuel prices alongside persistent wage pressures. Services companies also increased their selling prices at a somewhat faster pace, suggesting that cost pressures continued to be passed on to customers despite the still subdued pace of activity.

Central Bank of Kazakhstan Cuts Rates Again — The National Bank of Kazakhstan lowered its base rate by 25bps to 16.75% at its July 2026 meeting, following June's surprise 100bps cut, as annual inflation eased for a ninth consecutive month to 10.3% in June. Policymakers said disinflation continued to be supported by tight monetary policy, a stronger tenge, moderating consumer demand, and government anti-inflation measures, although recent monthly inflation data suggested the slowdown in price pressures may be losing momentum. The central bank also highlighted resilient economic activity, with GDP expanding 4.1% in the first half of 2026, supported by strong growth in construction, manufacturing, transportation, and investment. Policymakers reiterated that future interest rate decisions will remain data-dependent, as the balance of risks continues to be tilted toward inflation despite ongoing disinflation.

### 2026-07-24 15:35:14  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Services PMI #Importance 1

France Services Sector Hits 7-Month High — France's S&P Global Services PMI rose to 49.8 in July 2026, up from 46.8 in the previous month and exceeding market expectations of 47.5, signaling the softest contraction in business activity in seven months as the index moved closer to the 50.0 threshold. The latest reading was the highest since the outbreak of the Middle East conflict, reflecting a broad stabilization in the services sector. Demand improved, with new business expanding for the first time since November, supported by stronger domestic sales, although new export orders continued to decline sharply for an eighth consecutive month. Employment fell for a third straight month as firms remained reluctant to hire amid subdued business confidence, with some citing uncertainty ahead of next year's presidential election and the ongoing economic fallout from the Middle East conflict. On the price front, both input cost and output price inflation eased for a second straight month, further moderating price pressures.

The US launched a 13th straight day of strikes on Iran, with both sides ruling out near-term talks. President Trump also threatened "major military punishment" against Iran and the Houthis over any further attacks on Red Sea shipping and said he was considering a "massive attack" on Iran. Brent Up for Week Despite Friday Pullback — Brent crude oil slipped below $99 per barrel on Friday but remained up more than 12% for the week, as traders continued to assess mounting geopolitical risks to global energy supplies. The US launched a 13th straight day of strikes on Iran, with both sides ruling out near-term talks. President Trump also threatened "major military punishment" against Iran and the Houthis over any further attacks on Red Sea shipping and said he was considering a "massive attack" on Iran. Asian buyers have begun discussing rerouting Saudi crude shipments through the Suez Canal and around Africa. ### 2026-07-24 15:32:59  #Trading Economics #Economy #France #Composite PMI #Importance 1

France Private Sector Hits 5-Month High — France's S&P Global Flash Composite PMI rose to 49.6 in July 2026 from 47.2 in the previous month, signaling the softest contraction since the Middle East conflict began in February. The improvement was driven by the services sector, where activity rose to a seven-month high of 49.8 from 46.8 in June, supported by the first increase in demand since November. In contrast, manufacturing activity fell to 48.8 from 49.1, reflecting softer demand conditions. Meanwhile, private-sector employment fell for a third straight month as firms remained reluctant to hire. In addition, growing spare capacity was evident across France's private sector in July, with backlogs falling for a fourth straight month. On the price front, inflationary pressures eased, with both input costs and output prices rising at slower rates. Lastly, business confidence remained subdued, with the Future Output Index remaining well below its long-term average.

France Manufacturing Sector Stagnates in July — France's S&P Global Flash Manufacturing PMI fell to 50.0 in July 2026 from 51.2 in June, below market expectations of 51.0, flash estimates showed. The reading signaled stagnation in factory activity, as output declined at a faster pace, with the output index falling to 48.8 from 49.1 as firms cited weaker demand. New manufacturing orders also contracted at a quicker rate, while new export orders fell sharply for an eighth consecutive month. ### 2026-07-24 15:22:24  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Corn Hits Fresh 9-Week High — Corn futures rose above $4.6 per bushel, hitting a fresh nine-week high, supported by tightening global supply expectations, elevated crude oil prices, and adverse weather. Hot and dry conditions across parts of Europe and the Black Sea region continued to raise concerns over crop yields, while traders closely monitored weather across the US Corn Belt during the critical pollination stage. Additionally, crude oil prices remained near $100 per barrel amid escalating tensions in the Middle East, improving the outlook for corn-based ethanol demand. The market also remained underpinned by the USDA's latest WASDE report, which cut 2026/27 US ending stocks more than expected while raising export forecasts, pointing to a tighter domestic supply outlook. ### 2026-07-24 15:11:34  #MKT News #Commodities #Energy #Brent #Impact bearish #WTI

【Why Saudi seeks civilian nuclear despite ample oil】Saudi Arabia is pursuing civilian nuclear power to diversify an oil-dependent economy and free more crude for export. Oil accounted for about 40% of GDP in 2022 and Riyadh has run fiscal deficits in nine of the past ten years; the U.S.–Iran war exposed risks of heavy oil reliance. If permitted to enrich uranium—whether from domestic sources or imports—Saudi Arabia could use nuclear fuel to meet rising power demand and conserve fossil-fuel reserves for export. The program is framed as part of Vision 2030 and the green-modernization push (Riyadh pledged in 2021 to source half of electricity from renewables by 2030), and leaders may view enrichment-capable civilian nuclear as a first step toward regional deterrence and higher geopolitical status. Prolonged negotiations with Washington have also been used to deepen ties; Riyadh has repeatedly threatened to partner with Russia or others if U.S. terms prove unacceptable.

一、捷豹路虎（中國）投資有限公司

召回編號S2026M0079V：召回2018年4月11日至2020年7月13日生產的部分進口捷豹I-Pace汽車，共計854輛。

本次召回範圍內車輛的動力電池可能出現熱過載，極端情況下導致動力電池起火，存在安全隱患。

捷豹路虎（中國）投資有限公司自即日起為召回範圍內車輛實施階段性措施：升級電池能量控制模組（BECM）軟體，該軟體將車輛的充電上限設定為90%並監測動力電池執行狀態，當軟體監測出現預警提示時，將為車輛免費更換動力電池模組，以保證車輛正常安全行駛。捷豹路虎技術團隊正在開發進一步的解決方案，屆時，將為召回範圍內的車輛實施該方案，以消除安全隱患。

召回編號S2026M0080V：自2026年8月8日起，召回2020年12月7日至2026年5月7日期間生產的部分進口路虎攬勝、路虎發現汽車，共計76422輛。

召回編號S2026M0081V：自2026年8月8日起，召回2020年1月13日至2026年4月24日期間生產的部分進口路虎衛士汽車，共計83810輛。

### 2026-07-24 15:07:33  #Trading Economics #Economy #Spain #Producer Prices Change #Importance 1

Spain Producer Inflation Slows to 7% in June — Producer prices in Spain rose 7% year-on-year in June 2026, easing from a 10.5% jump in the previous month. Cost slowed for energy (14.3% vs 28.5% in May), due to lower prices in electricity production, transmission and distribution and a decline in oil refining prices; and consumer goods (1% vs 1.3%). On the other hand, prices accelerate for intermediate goods (6.6% vs 6.1%) and capital goods (2.5% vs 2.3%). Excluding energy, producer inflation picked up to 3.6% from 3.4% in the prior month. On a monthly basis, producer prices showed no growth, after a 1% increase in May.

【風暴導致SpaceX星艦第13次試飛再推遲 週五衝擊收官亞軌道任務】受伯莎熱帶風暴帶來的天氣影響，SpaceX取消了原定於當地時間週四進行的星艦第13次試飛發射計劃，將任務推遲至週五執行。該公司在X平臺上確認了此次延期，並表示：“受天氣條件限制，星艦第13次試飛現調整目標時間為7月24日（週五）。本次試飛一項核心目標是，在箭體上升至更高動壓區間時，從地面獲取星艦隔熱盾的清晰影像，而今日的天氣狀況無法完成該項拍攝。”本次第13次試飛是星艦飛船與超重助推器V3版本繼5月第12次試飛首飛後的第二次升空。本次任務搭載20顆量產版第三代星鏈（Starlink V3）運營衛星，這是SpaceX首次在星艦試飛中搭載正式服役衛星，而非以往的質量模擬配重體。其中6顆衛星搭載了攝像裝置，可在上升階段從另一視角對隔熱盾進行觀測，為工程師提供除地面拍攝（本次天氣受阻）之外的第二套資料來源。 若週五發射嘗試順利完成，第13次試飛或將是該專案最後一次亞軌道測試。SpaceX已規劃在第14次試飛直接挑戰入軌軌道級飛行。

### 2026-07-24 15:06:43  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Hungary Jobless Rate Unchanged at 4.5% in April–June — The unemployment rate in Hungary stood at 4.5% in the period from April to June 2026, unchanged from the corresponding period a year earlier. The number of unemployed individuals declined by 0.4 thousand from a year earlier to 218 thousand. Of the total unemployed, 106 thousand were men, with an unemployment rate of 4.1%, while 112 thousand were women, with an unemployment rate of 4.9%. Moreover, the average time spent looking for a job was 12.5 months. Among unemployed individuals, 36.7% had been seeking work for less than three months, while 28.0% had been looking for work for 4–11 months. The proportion of those searching for work for more than a year increased by 3.6 percentage points to 35.3%. Meanwhile, the number of employed persons decreased by 28 thousand year-on-year to 4.633 million. Employment among men increased by 1 thousand to 2.473 million, while employment among women decreased by 29 thousand to 2.160 million.

ASX 200 Ends Week Lower as Tech, Gold Miners Slide — Australia's ASX 200 dropped 67 points, or 0.75%, to finish at 8,772 on Friday, reversing gains from the previous three sessions as weakness spread across tech, consumer durables, non-energy minerals, and healthcare. Sentiment soured after Wall Street’s sharp tech sell-off the night before. For the week, the index shed 0.3%, marking a third consecutive weekly decline, with strong June jobs data reinforcing expectations of further tightening by the Reserve Bank after three hikes this year. Inflation figures for June and Q2, due next week, remain in focus amid persistent price concerns. Tech leaders Xero (-4.5%), Wisetech Global (-4.6%), and Megaport (-3.3%) led losses, while gold miners Northern Star (-3.9%) and Evolution (-2.4%) also retreated. In contrast, the big four banks advanced between 1.0% and 1.5%. Energy stocks also firmed as oil surged past USD 100 following U.S. President Donald Trump’s warning of “major military punishment” against Iran after Houthi-linked tanker attacks.

### 2026-07-24 14:57:33  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

UK Natural Gas Hovers at 4-Month High — UK natural gas prices climbed back to above 150 pence per therm on Friday, hovering at a four-month high, amid concerns over Europe’s LNG supply security as US-Iran fighting intensifies. The US carried out its 13th consecutive night of strikes on Iran, with President Donald Trump warning Tehran and its Houthi allies in Yemen of “major military punishment” if the group launches further attacks on vessels in the Red Sea. UK gas prices have risen over 9% so far this week and surged more than 44% so far in July, as curtailed LNG flows from the Persian Gulf intensified competition with Asian buyers, while hot weather across Europe boosted electricity demand for cooling. These factors are making it more challenging for Europe to replenish its gas storage facilities ahead of winter. Equinor, Europe’s largest gas supplier, said the bloc is unlikely to reach its 80% storage target before the heating season, leaving the market more vulnerable to sharp price swings when colder weather arrives.
